# Revenge of the Sith (Matthew Stover novelization): Power-Scaling Research Notes

## 9. Quote bank: every name/alias mention and attributed line, per major character (auto-harvested, verbatim)
Each line: chapter, line, kind, version tag, quote. **OWN LINE** = a paragraph spoken by the character (attribution method in parentheses: *tagged* = speech verb plus name; *beat* = the only named character in the paragraph's narration; *alternation* = inferred from two-speaker alternation, the least reliable). **SAID ABOUT** = a sentence of dialogue that names the character (speaker in parentheses when known). **NARRATION** = a narrated sentence that names the character. Long paragraphs are windowed around the name, so each quote is an exact substring.

### 9.0 Hand-curated pronoun-only and epithet-only lines (200 quotes)

Key lines about major and secondary characters that never name them in the sentence ("he", "the boy", "the shadow", "the creature", second-person "you"), so the name harvester below misses them. Each was hand-picked during the line-by-line read, and each is checked as an exact substring of its own source line as well as by the global verifier.

**Anakin Skywalker / Darth Vader** (55)

- Ch1 Anakin And Obi-Wan L319 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Not because of the overwhelming odds, or the danger he was in; he didn't care about odds, and he didn't think of himself as being in any particular danger.»
- Ch1 Anakin And Obi-Wan L321 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «In those days he had used the Force without knowing it; he'd thought the Force was something inside him, just a feeling, an instinct, a string of lucky guesses that led him through maneuvers other pilots wouldn't dare attempt.»
- Ch1 Anakin And Obi-Wan L357 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «He could whip any ten clever and subtle Jedi with his lightsaber tied behind his back.»
- Ch1 Anakin And Obi-Wan L443 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «He has not just power, not just skill, but dash: that rare, invaluable combination of boldness and grace.»
- Ch1 Anakin And Obi-Wan L443 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «The most powerful Jedi of his generation. Perhaps of any generation. The fastest. The strongest.»
- Ch1 Anakin And Obi-Wan L491 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Not the way the HoloNet labels him; not without fear, but stronger than fear.»
- Ch1 Anakin And Obi-Wan L497 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Because his real fear, in a universe where even stars can die, is that being the best will never be quite good enough.»
- Ch3 The Way Of The Sith L1555 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «There was a thermonuclear furnace where his heart should be, and it was burning through the firewalls of his Jedi training. He held the Force in the clench of a white-hot fist. He was half Sith already, and he didn't even know it.»
- Ch3 The Way Of The Sith L1557 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «This boy had the gift of fury.»
- Ch3 The Way Of The Sith L1561 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Dread, Dooku surmised, of himself. Of what might happen if he should ever allow that furnace he used for a heart to go supercritical.»
- Ch3 The Way Of The Sith L1567 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «The angrier he got, the more afraid he became, and the fear fed his anger in turn; like the proverbial Corellian multipede, now that he had started thinking about what he was doing, he could no longer walk.»
- Ch4 Jedi Trap L1597 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «fury is a blade that makes his lightsaber into a toy»
- Ch4 Jedi Trap L1609 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Only he stands between death and the two men he loves best in all the world, and he can no longer afford to hold anything back.»
- Ch4 Jedi Trap L1625 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Decision is reality, here: his blade moves simultaneously with his will and blue fire vaporizes black Corellian nanosilk and disintegrates flesh and shears bone, and away falls a Sith Lord's lightsaber hand, trailing smoke that tastes of charred meat and burned hair.»
- Ch4 Jedi Trap L1633 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Both lightsabers are in his hands, and the one in his hand of flesh flares with the synthetic bloodshine of a Sith blade.»
- Ch4 Jedi Trap L1755 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «In the Tusken camp he had lost his mind; he had become a force of nature, indiscriminate, killing with no more thought or intention than a sand gale.»
- Ch5 Grievous L1913 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «The Force was with him today in ways more potent than he had ever experienced.»
- Ch6 Rescue L2047 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «He fixed a measuring glance on the open lift doorway above; perhaps the Force could give him enough of a boost to carry all three of them to safety.»
- Ch7 Obi-Wan and Anakin 2 L2659 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Blowing up vulture droids and tri-fighters is simply a matter of superior reflexes and trust in the Force; he has spent so many hours in the cockpit that he wears a Jedi starfighter like clothes.»
- Ch7 Obi-Wan and Anakin 2 L2659 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Many people say he is the best star pilot in the galaxy, but that's merely talk, born of the constant HoloNet references to his unmatched string of kills in starfighter combat.»
- Ch7 Obi-Wan and Anakin 2 L2673 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «And at the same time, he draws power from the Force.»
- Ch8 Fault Lines L2735 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Presence.»
- Ch9 Padme L3093 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «A man who knew exactly what he wanted and was honest enough to simply ask for it; a man strong enough to unroll his deepest feelings before her without fear and without shame.»
- Ch9 Padme L3095 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «he is prideful, and moody, and quick to anger»
- Ch9 Padme L3097 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «He is a wild creature who has come gently to her hand, a vine tiger purring against her cheek.»
- Ch9 Padme L3293 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «He wanted to cry and he wanted to rage and he wanted to make his rage a weapon that would cut himself free forever.»
- Ch9 Padme L3295 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «He could show them something, though. He could show them a reason for their fear.»
- Ch10 Masters L3697 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «If they cannot control you now, what will happen once you are a Master in your own right?»
- Ch11 Politics L3905 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Behind his anger hid the dragon.»
- Ch11 Politics L3945 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «It didn't sound like him at all, and it smoked with fury.»
- Ch11 Politics L3953 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «It was the voice of Dooku's destroyer.»

- Ch15 Death on Utapau L5731 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «I know you burn for greater power than any Jedi can wield; give yourself permission to gain that power, and allow yourself license to use it.»

- Ch17 The Face of the Dark L6443 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Everywhere he looked, he saw only the face of the woman he loved beyond love: the woman for whom he channeled through his body all the love that had ever existed in the galaxy.»

- Ch17 The Face of the Dark L6905 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «He still felt them-if anything, they burned hotter than before-but they no longer had the power to cloud his mind.»

- Ch19 The Face of the Sith L7843 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «a black-gloved hand made a fist. The door crumpled and fell.»

- Ch21 A New Jedi Order L8867 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Darth Vader, post-Mustafar (maimed/suited), 19 BBY] «you are so far less now than what you were, you are more than half machine, you are like a painter gone blind, a composer gone deaf, you can remember where the power was but the power you can touch is only a memory»

- Ch15 Death on Utapau L5869 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «A single slash of his lightsaber amputated the shoulder cannon of one power droid and continued into a spinning Force-assisted kick that brought his boot heel to the point of the other power droid's duranium chin, snapping the droid's head back hard enough to sever its cervical sensor cables.»

- Ch15 Death on Utapau L6017 [PRONOUN/EPITHET] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «After all, the only form in which he was truly even proficient was Soresu, which was the most common lightsaber form in the Jedi Order.»

### 9.1 Anakin Skywalker / Darth Vader (1199 quotes: 300 own lines, 325 said-about, 574 narration)

**Introduction: The Age of Heroes**

- L87 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin and Obi-Wan will be there any minute.»
- L91 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin and Obi-Wan.»
- L91 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Kenobi and Skywalker.»
- L91 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «From the beginning of the Clone Wars, the phrase Kenobi and Skywalker has become a single word.»
- L93 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Even grown-ups are not immune; it's not uncommon for an exasperated parent to ask, when faced with offspring who have just tried to pull off one of the spectacularly dangerous bits of foolishness that are the stock-in-trade of high-spirited younglings everywhere, So which were you supposed to be, Kenobi or Skywalker?»
- L95 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Skywalker is the master of audacity; his intensity, boldness, and sheer jaw-dropping luck are the perfect complement to Kenobi's deliberate, balanced steadiness.»
- L97 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «All the younglings watching the battle in Coruscant's sky know it: when Anakin and Obi-Wan get there, those dirty Seppers are going to wish they'd stayed in bed today.»
- L99 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «That's part of what being a grown-up is: understanding that heroes are created by the HoloNet, and that the real-life Kenobi and Skywalker are only human beings, after all.»
- L111 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Not even Kenobi and Skywalker.»

**Ch1 Anakin And Obi-Wan**

- L153 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «R4-P17 had been spending too much time with Anakin's eccentric astromech; it was picking up R2-D2's bad habits.»
- L165 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «It seemed that only last week Anakin had been a ten-year-old who wouldn't stop pestering him about Form I lightsaber combat.»
- L175 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan kept hoping to hear some of Anakin's old cocky grin in his tone, but he never did.»
- L179 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «And Anakin still tried to answer.»
- L187 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan was already making that exact move as Anakin spoke.»
- L187 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «But they were inverted to each other: breaking right shot him one way while Anakin whipped the other.»
- L189 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin!»
- L201 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «But even the electronic reflexes of the tri-fighters' droid brains were too slow for this: one of his pursuers met one of Anakin's head-on.»
- L205 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Oh, marvelous," he muttered under his breath. Anakin's surviving pursuer had switched targets. "Why is it always me?"»
- L207 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Perfect." Through the cockpit speakers, Anakin's voice carried grim satisfaction. "Both of them are on your tail."»
- L211 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Break left." Anakin sounded calm as a stone. "The turbolaser tower off your port bow: thread its guns. I'll take things from there."»
- L215 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"I'm right behind you. Artoo, lock on."»
- L217 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, they're all over me!"»
- L219 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Dead ahead. Move right to clear my shot. Now!"»
- L221 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «One of the tri-fighters behind him decided it couldn't follow and went for a ventral slip that took it directly into the blasts from Anakin's cannons.»
- L225 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Good shooting, Artoo." Anakin's dry chuckle in the cockpit's speakers vanished behind the clang of lasers blasting ablative shielding off Obi-Wan's left wing.»
- L237 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «When Obi-Wan went left and Anakin right, the tri-fighter would swing halfway through the difference.»
- L237 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «It was averaging his movements with Anakin's; somehow its droid brain had realized that as long as it stayed between the two Jedi, Anakin couldn't fire on it without hitting his partner.»
- L253 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin, where are you?"»
- L257 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"I have the lock!" Anakin said. "Go! Firing-now!"»
- L259 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan hit maximum drag on his intact wing, and his starfighter shot into a barely controlled arc high and right as Anakin's cannons vaporized the last tri-fighter.»
- L261 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Thanks, Anakin.»
- L265 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «And, Anakin-?"»
- L285 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «And Obi-Wan Kenobi knows, too, that to have lived his life without being Master to Anakin Skywalker would have left him a different man.»
- L287 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin has taught him so much.»
- L289 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan sees so much of Qui-Gon in Anakin that sometimes it hurts his heart; at the very least, Anakin mirrors Qui-Gon's flair for the dramatic, and his casual disregard for rules.»
- L289 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Training Anakin-and fighting beside him, all these years-has unlocked something inside Obi-Wan.»
- L289 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «It's as though Anakin has rubbed off on him a bit, and has loosened that clenched-jaw insistence on absolute correctness that Qui-Gon always said was his greatest flaw.»
- L293 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Though he does not know it, his relationship with Anakin has molded him into the great Jedi Qui-Gon always said he might someday be.»
- L303 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «And he is proud to be Anakin Skywalker's best friend.»
- L307 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «A translation spidered across Anakin's console readout: SCANNING.»
- L315 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «For Anakin Skywalker, starfighter battles were usually as close to fun as he ever came.»
- L337 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin had been late once before.»
- L341 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin could see exactly the look on his former Master's face: a mask of calm belied by a jaw so tight that when he spoke his mouth barely moved. "Don't worry, Master. If his beacon's working, Artoo'll find it. Have you thought about how we'll find the Chancellor if-"»
- L345 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin had to stop himself from reminding Obi-Wan that he wasn't a Padawan anymore. "I should have been here," he said through his teeth. "I told you. I should have been here."»

- L349 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin assumed he was supposed to be impressed. "But General Grievous-"»
- L351 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Master Ti had faced him before, Anakin.»
- L353 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"I've noticed." He let it drop. The middle of a space battle was no place to get into this particular sore subject.»
- L355 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Instead, Anakin had been stuck running around the Outer Rim for months like some useless Padawan, and all Palpatine had for protectors were Jedi who were clever and subtle.»
- L361 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Put yourself in the moment, Anakin.»
- L363 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Copy that, Master," Anakin said dryly. "Focusing now."»
- L365 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «R2-D2 twittered, and Anakin checked his console readout.»
- L367 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, there are dozens of cruisers dead ahead!"»
- L379 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin-"»
- L383 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"No games, Anakin.»
- L383 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «There's too much at stake." There, that was the tone Anakin had been looking for: a slightly scolding, schoolmasterish edge.»
- L389 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"No joy, Master." Anakin's stomach clenched, but he fought the tension out of his voice. "We may be the only two Jedi out here."»

- L403 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin, wait-the cruiser's bay shields have dropped!»
- L405 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin's smile tightened.»
- L415 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Negative," Anakin said. "I'm going head-to-head. See you on the far side."»
- L417 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin-"»
- L419 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «He could hear the frustration in Obi-Wan's voice as he kicked his starfighter's sublights and surged past; his former Master still hadn't gotten used to not being able to order Anakin around.»
- L421 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Not that Anakin had ever been much for following orders.»
- L425 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, form up!"»
- L427 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «But Anakin was already streaking to meet the Trade Federation fighters. "Incoming!"»
- L429 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan's familiar sigh came clearly over the comm; Anakin knew exactly what the Jedi Master was thinking.»
- L433 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin's smile thinned to a grim straight line as enemy starfighters swarmed around him.»
- L441 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «This is Anakin Skywalker:»
- L451 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin sometimes thinks of the dread that eats at his heart as a dragon.»
- L453 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «But Anakin's fear is another kind of dragon.»
- L457 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin couldn't even remember what the mission might have been, but he'd never forgotten that dead star.»
- L463 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «That is a path of misery, Anakin; the Jedi do not walk it."»
- L465 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «That is the kind of fear that lives inside Anakin Skywalker: the dragon of that dead star.»
- L469 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «The dragon whispers of what Anakin has lost.»
- L471 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «The dragon reminds him, every night, of how he held his dying mother in his arms, of how she had spent her last strength to say I knew you would come for me, Anakin . . .»
- L475 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «All things die, Anakin Skywalker.»
- L487 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «The Supreme Chancellor has been family to Anakin: always there, always caring, always free with advice and unstinting aid.»
- L487 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «A sympathetic ear and a kindly, loving, unconditional acceptance of Anakin exactly as he is-the sort of acceptance Anakin could never get from another Jedi.»
- L491 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «And Anakin is on his way despite the dread boiling through his blood.»
- L495 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «If anyone can save Palpatine, Anakin will.»
- L499 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin whipped by him and used his forward attitude jets to kick himself into a skew-flip: facing backward to blast the last of the tri-fighters on his tail.»
- L507 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Watch this." Anakin flipped his starfighter again and dived, spinning, directly through the flock of vulture droids. Their drives blazed as they came around. He led them streaking for the upper deck of a laser-scarred Separatist cruiser. "I'm going to lead them through the needle."»
- L509 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Don't lead them anywhere." Obi-Wan's threat display tallied the vultures on Anakin's tail.»

- L519 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «He couldn't waste time going around; Anakin still had eight of them on his tail.»
- L527 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin's starfighter skimmed only meters above the cruiser's dorsal hull.»

- L537 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Don't try it, Anakin.»
- L543 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Easy, Artoo," Anakin said. "We've done this before."»
- L551 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"What do you expect me to do? Close my eyes and whistle?" Anakin muttered under his breath, then said aloud, "Copy that. Thinking now."»

- L651 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «The impact jolted Obi-Wan again, pounded a deep streak of dent into his starfighter's hull, and shattered the forward control surface of Anakin's wing.»
- L653 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin had forgotten the first principle of combat.»

- L671 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Not just Arfour. One of them jumped over when we hit."»
- L675 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Even flying blind and nearly out of control through the middle of a space battle, Obi-Wan could not avoid a second of disbelief at the bewildering variety of auxiliary tools and aftermarket behaviors Anakin had tinkered onto his starfighter's astromech, even beyond the sophisticated upgrades performed by the Royal Engineers of Naboo.»
- L677 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Then a panel opened in R2-D2's side and its datajack arm stabbed out and smacked the crippled buzz droid right off Anakin's hull.»
- L677 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «The buzz droid spun aft until it was caught in the blast wash of Anakin's sublights then blew away faster than even Obi-Wan's eye could follow.»

- L725 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin had hit him.»

Sometimes he thought he could almost understand the droid's electrosonic code. "Don't worry about us. Find Palpatine. Go on, I'll cover you."»
- L799 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin jumped ahead of it into a cascade of blasterfire and let the Force direct his blade.»
- L801 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Get to that link!" Anakin had to shout above the whine of blasters and the roar of exploding droids. "I'm going for Obi-Wan!"»
- L805 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin whirled to find Obi-Wan right behind him in the act of slicing neatly through the braincase of a battle droid.»
- L807 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"I appreciate the thought, Anakin," the Jedi Master said with a gentle smile.»
- L809 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «This, then, is Obi-Wan and Anakin:»
- L811 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Though Obi-Wan is sixteen standard years Anakin's elder, they have become men together.»
- L813 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «The war that has done this is not the Clone Wars; Obi-Wan and Anakin's war began on Naboo, when Qui-Gon Jinn died at the hand of a Sith Lord.»
- L817 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Say what you will about the wisdom of ancient Master Yoda, or the deadly skill of grim Mace Windu, the courage of Ki-Adi-Mundi, or the subtle wiles of Shaak Ti; the greatness of all these Jedi is unquestioned, but it pales next to the legend that has grown around Kenobi and Skywalker.»
- L823 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan and Anakin always answer.»
- L825 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Whether Obi-Wan's legendary cleverness might beat Anakin's raw power, straight up, no rules, is the subject of schoolyard fist-fights, creche-pool wriggle-matches, and pod-chamber stinkwars across the Republic.»
- L827 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin and Obi-Wan would never fight each other.»

- L929 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin started toward him. "Careful, Master, you'll hurt his feelings-" He stopped in his tracks, a curious look on his face as if he was trying to frown and to smile at the same time.»
- L931 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin?"»
- L963 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Lord Tyranus, Kenobi and Skywalker have arrived."»
- L995 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «The blue-scanned image before him now became miniatures of Kenobi and Skywalker as he had seen them so many times before: shoulder-to-shoulder, lightsabers whirling as they enthusiastically dismantled droid after droid after droid.»
- L1063 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Count Dooku watched with clinical distaste as the blue-scanned images of Kenobi and Skywalker engaged in a preposterous farce-chase, pursued by destroyer droids into and out of turbolift pods that shot upward and downward and even sideways.»
- L1101 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Kenobi and Skywalker were going through more low-comedy business with another balky turbolift-possibly Grievous having some fun with the shaft controls-while battle droids haplessly pursued.»
- L1117 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «His death may be the code key of the final lock that will seal Skywalker to us forever."»
- L1119 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Dooku understood: not only would the death of his mentor tip Skywalker's already unstable emotional balance down the darkest of slopes, but it would also remove the greatest obstacle to Skywalker's successful conversion.»
- L1119 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «As long as Kenobi was alive, Skywalker would never be securely in the camp of the Sith; Kenobi's unshakable faith in the values of the Jedi would keep the Jedi blindfold on Skywalker's eyes and the Jedi shackles on the young man's true power.»
- L1121 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"But I must ask, my Master: is Skywalker truly the man we want?"»
- L1139 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Skywalker's arm makes him, for our purposes, even better.»
- L1143 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Improving upon his Master's plan was near to impossible; his own idea, of substituting Kenobi for Skywalker, he had to admit was only the product of a certain misplaced sentimentality.»
- L1143 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Skywalker was almost certainly the man for the job.»
- L1149 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «He had no doubt that Skywalker would fall.»
- L1149 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Dooku understood that this was more than a test for Skywalker; though Sidious had never said so directly, Dooku was certain that he himself was being tested as well.»
- L1149 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Success today would show his Master that he was worthy of the mantle of Mastery himself: by the end of the coming battle, he would have initiated Skywalker into the manifold glories of the dark side, just as Sidious had initiated him.»
- L1153 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «But Kenobi having fallen to my blade, are you certain Skywalker will ever accept my orders?»
- L1155 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Skywalker's power brings with it more than mere obedience.»
- L1159 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «With his heroic capture of Count Dooku, Anakin Skywalker will become the ultimate hero: the greatest hero in the history of he Republic, perhaps of the Jedi Order itself.»
- L1181 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Kenobi and Skywalker are nearly at the door.»

**Ch3 The Way Of The Sith**

- L1195 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin pressed himself against the wall, a litter of saber-sliced droid parts around his feet.»
- L1199 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin's whole body hummed to the tune of his blue-hot blade.»
- L1201 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin."»
- L1203 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «He looked calm in a way Anakin could barely understand.»
- L1203 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «He gave a significant stare down at the lightsaber in Anakin's hand.»
- L1203 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, rescue," he said softly.»
- L1205 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin kept his weapon right where it was. "And Dooku?"»
- L1209 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin's mechanical fingers tightened until the grip of his lightsaber creaked. "I'd rather do it by hand."»
- L1213 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin.»
- L1215 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Heat rose in Anakin's cheeks. "I am not-" your Padawan anymore snarled inside his head, but that was adrenaline talking; he bit back the words and said instead, "-going to let you down, Master. Or Chancellor Palpatine."»
- L1219 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Yes." Anakin let his blade shrink away and moved past Obi-Wan into the turbolift lobby.»
- L1221 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin . . . ," Obi-Wan began warningly.»
- L1227 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Is distraction. I know. And I know that hope is as hollow as fear." Anakin let himself smile, just a bit. "And I know everything else you're dying to tell me right now."»
- L1231 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin's smile broadened toward a soft chuckle. "I think that's the first time you've ever admitted it."»
- L1233 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «They stopped at the the door to the General's Quarters: a huge oval of opalescent iridiite chased with gold. Anakin stared at his ghostly almost-reflection while he reached into the room beyond with the Force, and let the Force reach into him. "I'm ready, Master."»
- L1239 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin didn't look at him; he stared into the door, through he door, searching in its shimmering depths for a hint of an unguessable future.»
- L1243 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin." Obi-Wan's voice had gone soft, and his hand was warm on Anakin's arm.»
- L1245 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin turned, and found within Obi-Wan's eyes a depth of feeling he had only rarely glimpsed in all their years together; and the pure uncomplicated love that rose up within him then felt like a promise from the Force itself.»
- L1251 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Obi-Wan," Anakin said, "let's go get the Chancellor."»
- L1255 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Inside a turbolift pod, Dooku watched hologrammic images of Kenobi and Skywalker cautiously pick their way down the curving stairs from the entrance balcony to the main level of the General's Quarters, moving slowly to stay braced against the pitching of the cruiser.»
- L1273 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin slid along the bank of chairs on one side of the immense situation table that dominated the center of the General's Quarters' main room; Obi-Wan mirrored him on the opposite side.»
- L1277 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin caught Obi-Wan's eye across the table and nodded toward the dark shape ahead.»
- L1279 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin's mouth compressed.»
- L1283 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin froze.»
- L1287 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «And worse-Anakin saw in the Chancellor's face something he'd never dreamed he'd find there, and it squeezed breath from his lungs and wiped words from his brain.»
- L1291 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin didn't know what to say.»
- L1297 [SAID ABOUT (by Palpatine / Darth Sidious)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, behind you-!"»
- L1299 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin didn't turn.»
- L1315 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin Skywalker.»
- L1317 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Now Anakin didn't have any troubles at all.»
- L1329 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Skywalker gave Dooku only his back, but his blade was already out and his tall, lean frame stood frozen with anticipation: so motionless he almost seemed to shiver.»
- L1333 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Dooku derived a certain melancholy satisfaction-a pleasurably lonely contemplation of his own unrecognized greatness-from a brief reflection that Skywalker would never understand how much thought and planning, how much work, Lord Sidious had invested in so hastily orchestrating his sham victory.»
- L1349 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Skywalker was a storm cloud, flickering with dangerous lightning, building the rotation that threatens a tornado.»
- L1359 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Now Skywalker turned, meeting Dooku's direct gaze for the first time since the abandoned hangar on Geonosis.»
- L1363 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin-" In the Force, Dooku could feel Kenobi's disapproval of Skywalker's boasting; and he could also feel Kenobi's effortless self-restraint in focusing on the matter at hand.»
- L1365 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Dooku's sharp eye picked up the tightening of Skywalker's droid hand on his lightsaber's grip.»
- L1377 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Skywalker brought his lightsaber to a Shien ready: hand of black-gloved durasteel cocked high at his shoulder, blade angling upward and away. "This is a little more than an attempt."»
- L1385 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Skywalker mirrored him, swinging wide toward Dooku's flank.»
- L1389 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Don't-" The thunderstorm that was Skywalker in the Force boiled with sudden power. "Don't even speak her name."»
- L1391 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «The lad's personal issues were too tiresome to pursue; he knew far too much already about Skywalker's messy private life.»
- L1393 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"You mean destroy it.""»
- L1397 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Now, there's a coincidence," Kenobi replied dryly as he swung around Dooku to place the Count precisely between Skywalker and himself.»

- L1409 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Isolate Skywalker, slaughter Kenobi.»
- L1409 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Beyond that, it would be merely a matter of spinning Skywalker up into enough of a frenzy to break through his Jedi restraint and reveal the infinite vista of Sith power.»
- L1419 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «The ship shuddered and the red smoke surged from Anakin's spine into his arms and legs and head and when Dooku gave the slightest glance of concern over his shoulder, distracted for half an instant, Anakin just couldn't wait anymore.»

- L1427 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin launched himself at Dooku's back-and the Count half turned, gesturing casually while holding Obi-Wan at bay with an elegant one-handed bind.»

Dooku neatly sidestepped, cutting at the boy's leg, yet Skywalker's blade met the cut as he passed and he managed to sweep his blade behind his head to slap aside the casual thrust Dooku aimed at the back of his neck-but his clumsy charge had»
- L1449 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Kenobi drove a slash at the scarlet blade while he pivoted in the air, and again Dooku sidestepped so that now it was Kenobi in Skywalker's way.»
- L1455 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «It was a simple matter of countering their tactics, which were depressingly straightforward; Skywalker was the swift one, whooshing here and there like a spastic hawk-bat-attempting a Jedi variant of neek-in-the-middle so they could come at him from both sides-while Kenobi came on»
- L1473 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «And in the space where Kenobi's chest had been was now only the blue lightning of Skywalker's blade driving straight for Dooku's heart.»
- L1479 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «toward Kenobi's face, then dropped and spun in a reverse ankle-sweep-But not only did Kenobi easily overleap this attack, Dooku nearly lost his own foot to a slash from Skywalker who had again come out of nowhere and now carved through the table so that it collapsed under Dooku's weight and dumped the Sith Lord un-:eremoniously to the floor.»
- L1479 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Skywalker slammed his following strike down so hard that the shock of deflecting it buckled Dooku's elbows.»
- L1479 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Only a desperate whirling slash-block, coupled with a wheel kick that caught Kenobi on the thigh, bought him enough time to leap away again, and when he touched down-Skywalker was already there.»
- L1481 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «The first overhand chop of Skywalker's blade slid off Dooku's instinctive guard.»
- L1483 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Skywalker came on, mechanically inexorable, impossibly powerful, a destroyer droid with a lightsaber: each step a blow and each blow a step.»
- L1483 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Dooku backed away as fast as he dared; Skywalker stayed right on top of him.»
- L1483 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «He no longer tried to block Skywalker's strikes but only to guide them slanting away; he could not meet Skywalker strength-to-strength-not only did the boy wield tremendous reserves of Force energy, but his sheer physical power was astonishing-And only then did Dooku understand that he'd been suckered.»
- L1485 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Skywalker's Shien ready-stance had been a ruse, as had his Ataro gymnastics; the boy was a Djem So stylist, and as fine a one as Dooku had ever seen.»
- L1489 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «He dropped low and spun into another reverse ankle-sweep-the weakness of Djem So was its lack of mobility-that slapped Skywalker's boot sharply enough to throw the young Jedi off balance, giving Dooku the opportunity to leap away-Only to find himself again facing the wheel of blue lightning that was Kenobi's blade.»
- L1495 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «more than was necessary, deflecting without effort, riposting with flickering strikes and stabs swifter than the tongue of a Garollian ghost viper, and when Dooku felt Skywalker regain his feet and stride once more toward his back, he finally registered the source of that blinding defensive velocity Kenobi had used a moment ago, and only then, belatedly,»
- L1509 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Skywalker was all over him.»
- L1511 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «The shining blue lightsaber whirled and spat and every overhand chop crashed against Dooku's defense with the unstoppable power of a meteor strike; the Sith Lord spent lavishly of his reserve of the Force merely to meet these attacks without being cut in half, and Skywalker-Skywalker was getting stronger.»
- L1517 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «He retreated up them, using the higher ground for leverage, but Skywalker just kept on coming, tirelessly ferocious.»
- L1519 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «faster until Dooku saw the room through an electric haze and now Kenobi was back in the picture: with a shout of the Force, he shot like a torpedo up the stairs behind Skywalker, and Dooku decided that under these rather extreme circumstances, it was at least arguably permissible for a gentleman to cheat.»
- L1523 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Energy hammered out from the heavy blasters built into their arms; Skywalker whirled and his blade batted every blast back at the droids, whose mirror-polished carapace armor deflected the bolts again.»
- L1525 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Before their pieces could even hit the floor Dooku was in motion, landing a spinning side-stamp that folded Skywalker in half; he used his last burst of dark power to continue his spin into a blindingly fast wheel-kick that brought his heel against the point of Kenobi's chin with a crack like the»
- L1535 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Now, as for Skywalker-Which was as far as Dooku got, because by the time his attention returned to the younger Jedi, his vision was rather completely obstructed by the sole of a boot approaching his face with something resembling terminal velocity.»
- L1541 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «He dusted himself off and fixed a supercilious gaze on Skywalker, who now stood upon the balcony looking down at him-and Dooku couldn't hold the stare; he found this reversal of their original positions oddly unsettling.»
- L1543 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Seeing Skywalker standing where Dooku himself had stood only moments ago ... it was as though he was trying to remember a dream he'd never actually had . . .»
- L1549 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Skywalker leapt from the balcony.»
- L1551 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «He understood how Skywalker was getting stronger.»
- L1553 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Skywalker was a natural.»
- L1559 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «And even now, he was holding himself back; even now, as he landed at Dooku's flank and rained blows upon the Sith Lord's defenses, even as he drove Dooku backward step after step, Dooku could feel how Skywalker kept his fury banked behind walls of will: walls that were hardened by some uncontrollable dread.»
- L1563 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «You're a fraud, Skywalker.»
- L1567 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Skywalker leapt for him again, and this time Dooku met the boy's charge easily.»
- L1567 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «They stood nearly toe-to-toe, blades flashing faster than the eye could see, but Skywalker had lost his edge: a simple taunt was all that had been required to shift the focus of his attention from winning the fight to controlling his own emotions.»
- L1569 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Dooku allowed himself to relax; he felt that spirit of playfulness coming over him again as he and Skywalker spun 'round each other in their lethal dance.»
- L1573 [SAID ABOUT (by Palpatine / Darth Sidious)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Don't fear what you're feeling, Anakin, use it!" he barked in Palpatine's voice.»
- L1577 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «He and Skywalker paused for one single, final instant, blades locked together, staring at each other past a sizzling cross of scarlet against blue, and in that instant Dooku found himself wondering in bewildered astonishment if Sidious had suddenly lost his mind.»
- L1579 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «And through the cross of their blades he saw in Skywalker's eyes the promise of hell, and he felt a sickening presentiment that he already knew the answer to that question.»

**Ch4 Jedi Trap**

- L1587 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «A starburst of clarity blossoms within Anakin Skywalker's mind, when he says to himself Oh.»
- L1595 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Dooku & Skywalker, a one-time-only command performance, for an audience of one.»
- L1597 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «And all for nothing, because a nuclear flame has consumed Anakin Skywalker's Jedi restraint, and fear becomes fury without effort, and fury is a blade that makes his lightsaber into a toy.»
- L1607 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «But for Anakin, in the fight there is only terror, and rage.»
- L1609 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «that Dooku has all the power of the darkness, to remind him how Dooku took his hand, how Dooku could strike down even Obi-Wan himself seemingly without effort and now Anakin is all alone and he will never be a match for any Lord of the Sith-But Palpatine's words rage is your weapon have given Anakin permission to unseal the shielding around his furnace»
- L1611 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «When Count Dooku flies at him, blade flashing, Watto's fist cracks out from Anakin's childhood to knock the Sith Lord tumbling back.»
- L1613 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «When with all the power that the dark side can draw from throughout the universe, Dooku hurls a jagged fragment of the durasteel table, Shmi Skywalker's gentle murmur I knew you would come for me, Anakin smashes it aside.»
- L1615 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «cell of life in the infinite sterile desert of space, his firewalls have opened so that the terror and the rage are out there, in the fight instead of in his head, and Anakin's mind is clear as a crystal bell.»
- L1625 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «The hand falls with a bar of scarlet blaze still extending from its spastic death grip, and Anakin's heart sings for the fall of that red blade.»
- L1629 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «And then Anakin takes Dooku's other hand as well.»
- L1631 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Dooku crumples to his knees, face blank, mouth slack, and his weapon whirs through the air to the victor's hand, and Anakin finds his vision of the future happening before his eyes: two blades at Count Dooku's throat.»
- L1635 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Dooku, cringing, shrinking with dread, still finds some hope in his heart that he is wrong, that Palpatine has not betrayed him, that this has all been proceeding according to plan-Until he hears "Good, Anakin!»
- L1639 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «In Skywalker's eyes he sees only flames.»
- L1651 [SAID ABOUT (by Palpatine / Darth Sidious)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin," Palpatine says quietly.»
- L1653 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Years of Jedi training make Anakin hesitate; he looks down upon Dooku and sees not a Lord of the Sith but a beaten, broken, cringing old man.»
- L1657 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Now!" Anakin realizes that this isn't actually an order.»
- L1661 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «And Dooku-As he looks up into the eyes of Anakin Skywalker for the final time, Count Dooku knows that he has been deceived not just today, but for many, many years.»
- L1669 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «To be the victim of Anakin Skywalker's first cold-blooded murder.»
- L1697 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Dooku's severed staring head bounced along the deck and rolled away, and Anakin woke up.»
- L1713 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"You did well, Anakin." Palpatine's voice was warm as an arm around Anakin's shoulders.»
- L1715 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «From the Chancellor this sounded true, but when Anakin repeated it inside his head he knew that Palpatine's truth would be one he could never make himself believe.»
- L1735 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin shook his head. "That doesn't matter. It's not the Jedi way."»
- L1741 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin looked toward the shadow.»
- L1743 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, listen to me.»
- L1747 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"It wasn't wrong, Anakin.»
- L1751 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Don't be childish, Anakin.»
- L1763 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Yes-yes, of course, Chancellor, but-" Anakin wanted to crawl away into a corner somewhere; he felt sure that if things would just stop for a while-an hour, a minute-he could pull himself together and find some way to keep moving forward. He had to keep moving forward. Moving forward was all he could do»
- L1769 [SAID ABOUT (by Palpatine / Darth Sidious)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, my restraints, please," the shadow said.»
- L1771 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «In the Force, the field-signatures of the magnetic locks on the Chancellor's shackles were as clear as text saying unlock me like this; a simple twist of Anakin's mind popped them open.»
- L1773 [SAID ABOUT (by Palpatine / Darth Sidious)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Come along, Anakin.»
- L1775 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «The view wall flared white with the missiles' impacts, and one of them must have damaged the gravity generators: the ship seemed to heel over, forcing Palpatine to clutch desperately at the banister and sending Anakin skidding down a floor that had suddenly become a forty-five-degree ramp.»
- L1781 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Bad as Obi-Wan looked, Anakin had stood over the bodies of too many friends on too many battlefields to be panicked by a lit-tle blood.»
- L1781 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «One touch to Obi-Wan's throat confirmed the strength of his pulse, and that touch also let Anakin's Force perception flow through the whole body of his friend.»
- L1785 [SAID ABOUT (by Palpatine / Darth Sidious)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Leave him, Anakin.»
- L1787 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Then we'll all be adrift together." Anakin glanced up at the Supreme Chancellor and for that instant he didn't like the man at all-but then he reminded himself that brave as Palpatine was, his was the courage of conviction; the man was no soldier.»
- L1787 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «He had no way of truly comprehending what he was asking Anakin to do.»
- L1791 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «With Obi-Wan unconscious and Palpatine waiting above, with responsibility for the lives of his two closest friends squarely upon him, Anakin found that he had recovered his inner balance.»
- L1799 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Before Anakin could answer, the erratic gravity swung like a pendulum; while they both clung to the railing, the room seemed to roll around them.»

**Ch5 Grievous**

- L1875 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Over Anakin's shoulder, the unconscious Jedi Master wheezed faintly.»

- L1891 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin was familiar with the specs for Trade Federation command cruisers; the angled conning spire was some three hundred meters long.»

- L1907 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"But the bridge-Grievous is there." Now Anakin did stop.»
- L1907 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «In all the excitement, Anakin had entirely forgotten that the bio-droid general was aboard.»
- L1911 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin thought blankly: I could do it.»
- L1913 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «So close Anakin could practically smell him . . . and Anakin had never felt so powerful.»
- L1915 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Think of it, Anakin." Palpatine stood close by his shoulder, opposite to Obi-Wan, so close he needed only to whisper.»

**Ch9 Padme**

- L3087 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «She is Anakin Skywalker's wife.»
- L3089 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «For Padme Amidala, saying 1 am Anakin Skywalker's wife is saving neither more nor less than I am alive.»
- L3091 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Her life before Anakin belonged to someone else, some lesser being to be pitied, some poor impoverished spirit who could never suspect how profoundly life should be lived.»
- L3093 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Her real life began the first time she looked into Anakin Sky-walker's eyes and found in there not the uncritical worship of little Annie from Tatooine, but the direct, unashamed, smoldering passion of a powerful Jedi: a young man, to be»
- L3111 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «She is more, now, than Anakin Skywalker's wife.»
- L3113 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «She is the mother of Anakin Skywalker's unborn child.»
- L3119 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, Anakin, oh my Anakin, II can't believe you're home.»
- L3135 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «His arms were so strong, and so warm, and his hand touched her hair in the softest caress, as though he was afraid she were as fragile as a dream, and he bent down for another kiss, a new kiss, a kiss that would wipe away every dark dream and all the days and hours and minutes of unbearable dread-But only steps away, the main vault of the Atrium»
- L3139 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"No, Anakin.»
- L3143 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin-" He could still make her angry without even trying.»
- L3143 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «You are the face of the Jedi, Anakin.»
- L3145 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"And it always comes back to politics for you," Anakin said. His smile had gone now. "I'm barely even home, and you're already trying to talk me into going back to the war-"»

- L3153 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, listen-"»
- L3157 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin, stop.»

- L3547 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «When a Jedi had a question about the deepest subtleties of the Force, there was one source to whom he could always turn; and so, first thing that morning, without even taking time to stop by his own quarters for a change of clothing, Anakin had gone to Yoda for advice.»

- L3551 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «But this morning, despite clearly having other things on his mind-even Anakin's Force perceptions, far from the most subtle had detected echoes of conflict and worry within the Masster's chamber-Yoda had simply offered Anakin a place on one of the softly rounded pod seats and suggested that they meditate together.»
- L3555 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin had been so grateful-and so relieved, and so unexpectedly hopeful-that he'd found tears welling into his eyes, and some few minutes had been required for him to compose himself into proper Jedi serenity.»
