- L3577 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Of one thing Anakin was certain: the ancient Master had never been in love.»
- L3579 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Or he would have known better than to expect Anakin to just fold his hands and close his eyes and settle in to meditate while what was left of Padme's life evaporated like the ghost-mist of dew in a Tatooine winter dawn . . .»
- L3589 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Just being on its rooftop landing deck sent a surge of power through Anakin's whole body; if the Force was ever to show him a way to change the dark future of his nightmares, it would do so here.»
- L3591 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «It was there Anakin hoped to find everything that was known about prophetic dreams-and everything that was known about preventing these prophecies from coming to pass.»
- L3597 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «But now here was Obi-Wan-Obi-Wan would help him, Anakin knew he would-if only Anakin could figure out the right way to ask . . .»
- L3601 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"I-was held up," Anakin said. "I have no excuse."»
- L3605 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Is Palpatine here?" Anakin asked. It was a convenient-enough way to change the subject. "Has something happened?"»
- L3609 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Waiting? For we?" Anakin frowned. Worries and lack of sleep had his head full of fog; he couldn't make this make sense. He patted his robes vacantly. "But-my beacon hasn't gone off. If the Council wanted me, why didn't they-"»
- L3613 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"I don't understand."»
- L3617 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Why wouldn't he go through the Council?"»
- L3621 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «A queasy knot began to tie itself behind Anakin's ribs. "Obi-Wan, what's going on? Something's wrong, isn't it? You know something, I can tell."»
- L3625 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin remembered what he'd said to Padme about exactly that last night. The queasy knot tightened. "And?"»
- L3627 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"And that's why I am out here, Anakin.»
- L3629 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «" What conversation? I still don't know what's going on!"»
- L3631 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Not really." Obi-Wan put a hand on Anakin's shoulder and frowned deeply into his eyes.»
- L3631 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, you know I am your friend."»
- L3633 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Of course you are-"»
- L3635 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «No of courses, Anakin.»
- L3637 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"What do you mean?"»
- L3639 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Be careful of him, Anakin.»
- L3641 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Careful? Don't you mean, mindful?"»
- L3643 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Please, Anakin-please be careful"»
- L3645 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin tried for his old rakish smile. "You worry too much."»
- L3649 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"-because I don't worry at all, right?" Anakin finished for him.»
- L3653 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"You're wrong, you know." Anakin stared off through the morning haze toward the shuttle, past the shuttle-Toward 500 Republica, and Padme's apartment. He said, "I worry plenty."»
- L3655 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin had tried making conversation with the two tall helmet-masked figures in the red robes, but they weren't exactly chatty.»
- L3657 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin's discomfort only increased when he arrived at Palpatine's office.»
- L3667 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin." He must have seen Anakin's reflection in the curve of transparisteel; he had not moved. "Join me."»
- L3669 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin came up beside him, mirroring his stance.»
- L3671 [SAID ABOUT (by Palpatine / Darth Sidious)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Do you see, Anakin?" Palpatine's voice was soft, hoarse with emotion.»
- L3673 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Gently, Anakin laid a hand on Palpatine's shoulder, and a hint of frown fleeted over his face at how frail seemed the flesh and bone beneath the robe.»
- L3675 [SAID ABOUT (by Palpatine / Darth Sidious)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"I know I have yours, Anakin.»
- L3677 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «I hope I can depend upon your discretion, Anakin."»
- L3679 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin went still for a moment, then he very slowly lifted his hand from the Chancellor's shoulder.»
- L3685 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, in all the years we have been friends, have I ever asked you to do anything even the slightest bit against your conscience?"»
- L3689 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «I am very proud of your accomplishments as a Jedi, Anakin.»
- L3693 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «They keep you off the Council because it is the last hold they have on you, Anakin; it is how they control you.»
- L3695 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Well . . ." Anakin gave him a half-sheepish smile. "They can't exactly make me, even now."»
- L3699 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin looked down. This had struck a little close to the bone. "I have sensed . . . something like that."»
- L3701 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"I have asked you here today, Anakin, because I have fears of my own." He turned, waiting, until Anakin met his eye, and on Palpatine's face was something approaching bleak despair.»
- L3703 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Oh, Chancellor-" Anakin broke into a smile of disbelief. "There is no one more loyal than the Jedi, sir-surely, after all this time-"»
- L3705 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «He lowered himself into the chair behind his desk and kept his head down as though he was ashamed to say this directly to Anakin's face.»
- L3711 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Or had they been calculated: seeds of doubt planted to hedge Anakin away from the one man who really understood him?»
- L3721 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"I . . ." Anakin frowned. How could he be sure? "But Sidious . . ."»
- L3723 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"The Sith infiltrator in the highest levels of government.'' Doesn't that sound a little overly familiar to you, Anakin?»
- L3727 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"In a democracy, everything is political, Anakin.»
- L3731 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «I have been reading about the history of the Sith for some years now, Anakin.»
- L3739 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"You would-you would-" Anakin couldn't quite make himself believe what he was hearing. The blood-red rug beneath his feet seemed to shift under him, and his head was starting to spin.»
- L3743 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"You-Chancellor, you can't be serious-"»
- L3745 [SAID ABOUT (by Palpatine / Darth Sidious)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"It's only an example, Anakin.»
- L3747 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"I wouldn't know," Anakin said feelingly, "but I do know that you probably shouldn't use that . . . example ... in front of the Jedi Council."»
- L3751 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"I'm sure they'd never do that-"»
- L3753 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «I am no longer sure they'll stop at anything, Anakin.»
- L3755 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin's frown deepened. "The Jedi will no longer report to the Senate?"»
- L3759 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin nodded.»
- L3779 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, I am asking you-as a personal favor to me, in respect for our long friendship-to accept a post as my personal representative on the Jedi Council." Anakin blinked. He blinked again. He said, "Me?"»
- L3785 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"On the Council . . . ," Anakin murmured.»

**Ch11 Politics**

- L3815 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"We can always trust Anakin to do what he thinks is right.»
- L3831 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «I have told you all what I have seen of the energy between Skywalker and the Supreme Chancellor.»
- L3831 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anything that might distance young Skywalker from Palpatine's influence is worth the attempt."»
- L3837 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «We have seen that young Skywalker has the power to battle a Sith Lord alone, if need be; he has proven that with Dooku.»
- L3839 [SAID ABOUT (by Yoda)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"And even if the prophecy has been misread," Agen Kolar added, "Anakin is the one Jedi we can best hope would survive an encounter with a Sith Lord.»
- L3839 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin will certainly report this to the Chancellor's Office.»
- L3849 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"And one last touch Let's let the Chancellor know, through Anakin, that our most cunning and insightful Master-and our most tenacious-is to lead the hunt for Grievous."»
- L3895 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «By the time the doors to the Jedi Council Chamber finally swung open, Anakin was already angry.»
- L3901 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin walked into the Council Chamber, head lowered in a show of humility and respect.»
- L3921 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin Skywalker." Master Windu's tone was so severe that the dragon inside Anakin coiled instinctively.»
- L3923 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin stood very still for a long moment, until he could be absolutely sure he had heard what he thought he'd heard.»
- L3925 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «In fact-now that Anakin came to think of it-he couldn't remember a single instance when the Supreme Chancellor had been wrong.»
- L3931 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Allow this appointment lightly, the Council does not." Yoda's ears curled forward at Anakin like accusing fingers.»
- L3935 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin inclined his head. "I understand."»
- L3937 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"I'm not sure you do." Mace Windu leaned forward, staring into Anakin's eyes with a measuring squint.»
- L3939 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin was barely paying attention; in his mind, he was already leaving the Council Chamber, riding the turbolift to the archives, demanding access to the restricted vault by authority of»
- L3949 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin stood welded to the floor, motionless.»
- L3961 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Mace Windu's eyes were as cold as the voice from Anakin's mouth.»
- L3961 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Take your seat, young Skywalker."»
- L3963 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin matched his stare.»
- L3963 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Go ahead and Vaapad this, you-"Anakin," Obi-Wan said softly.»
- L3965 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «And something in Obi-Wan's gentle voice, in his simple, straightforward request, sent his anger slinking off ashamed, and Anakin found himself alone on the carpet in the middle of the Jedi Council, blinking.»
- L3969 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «The rest of the session passed in a haze; Ki-Adi-Mundi said something about no Republic world reporting any sign of Grievous, and Anakin felt a dull shock when the Council assigned the task of coordinating the search to Obi-Wan alone.»
- L3975 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Skywalker, your assignment is here.'" Mace Windu's stare was hard as durasteel, and only a scrape short of openly hostile.»
- L3981 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «And as the holopresences of Plo Koon and Ki-Adi-Mundi winked out, as Obi-Wan and Agen Kolar rose and spoke together in tones softly grave, as Yoda and Mace Windu walked from the room, Anakin could only sit, sick at heart, stunned with helplessness.»
- L4023 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «No matter how much it hurt Anakin.»
- L4039 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «And all she could think as the little group dispersed to their own offices was Oh, Anakin-Anakin, I'm sorry . . .»
- L4043 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin was glad the vast vaulted Temple hallway was deserted save for him and Obi-Wan; he didn't have to keep his voice down.»
- L4057 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin flung an arm back toward the Council Tower. "They're the ones who call me the chosen one! Chosen for what? To be a dupe in some slimy political game?"»
- L4059 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Didn't I warn you, Anakin?»
- L4061 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Like that ray shield trap." Anakin snorted. "Should I blame this on the dark side, too?"»
- L4065 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"What situation? Who cares about me? I'm no Master, I'm just a kid, right? Is that what it's about? Is Master Windu turning everyone against me because until I came along, he was the youngest Jedi ever named to the Council?"»
- L4069 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Sure they don't. Let me tell you something a smart old man said to me no so long ago: Age is no measure of wisdom. If it were, Yoda would be twenty times as wise as you are-"»
- L4073 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"That's right. It has to do with me. It has to do with them all being against me. They always have been-most of them didn't even want me to be a Jedi. And if they'd won out, where would they be right now? Who would have done the things I've done? Who would have saved Naboo? Who would have saved Kamino? Who would have killed Dooku, and rescued the»
- L4075 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Yes, Anakin, yes.»
- L4077 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"I'm too close to him? Maybe I am. Maybe I should alienate a man who's been nothing but kind and generous to me ever since I first came to this planet! Maybe I should reject the only man who gives me the respect I deserve-"»
- L4079 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, stop.»
- L4079 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «These are dark thoughts, Anakin.»
- L4081 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin passed his flesh hand over his eyes and drew a long, heavy breath. In a much lower, calmer, quieter tone, he said, "What do I have to do?"»
- L4085 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"They want something from me, don't they? That's what this is really about. That's what it's been about from the beginning. They won't give me my rank until I give them what they want."»
- L4087 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"The Council does not operate that way, Anakin, and you know it."»
- L4091 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Yes, I know it. Sure I do," Anakin said. Suddenly he was tired. So incredibly tired. It hurt to talk. It hurt even to stand here. He was sick of the whole business. Why couldn't it just be over? "Tell me what they want."»
- L4093 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan's eyes shifted, and the sick fatigue in Anakin's guts turned darker.»
- L4095 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, look, I'm on your side," Obi-Wan said softly.»
- L4095 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «He looked tired, too: he looked as tired and sick as Anakin felt.»
- L4099 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin said, "Look, whatever it is, it's not getting any better while you're standing here working up the nerve to tell me. Come on, Obi-Wan. Let's have it."»
- L4101 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan glanced around the empty hall as if he wanted to make sure they were still alone; Anakin had a feeling it was just an excuse to avoid facing him when he spoke.»
- L4105 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"They want me to spy on the Supreme Chancellor of the Republic?" Anakin blinked numbly.»
- L4107 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"We are at war, Anakin." Obi-Wan looked thoroughly miserable.»
- L4109 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin's eyes narrowed and turned hard. "Why didn't the Council give me this assignment while we were in session?"»
- L4111 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Because it's not for the record, Anakin.»
- L4113 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"What I understand," Anakin said grimly, "is that you are trying to turn me against Palpatine. You're trying to make me keep secrets from him-you want to make me lie to him. That's what this is really about."»
- L4117 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"He's not a bad man, Obi-Wan-he's a great man, who's holding this Republic together with his bare hands-"»
- L4121 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"The Senate demanded that he stay! They pushed those powers on him-"»
- L4125 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Then it's their fault, not his! They should have the guts to stand up to him!"»
- L4127 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"That is what we're asking you to do, Anakin."»
- L4129 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin had no answer.»
- L4141 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"You know how kind he has been to me." Anakin's voice was hushed. "You know how he's looked after me, how he's done everything he could to help me. He's like family.''''»
- L4145 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"No." Anakin turned on his former Master. "No, the Jedi are your family. The only one you've ever known. But I'm not like you-I had a mother who loved me-"»
- L4153 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «All things die, Anakin Skywalker.»
- L4155 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, yes.»
- L4157 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan stepped closer and put a hand on Anakin's arm.»
- L4165 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin said, "I can keep a secret."»
- L4171 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «What we are asking of you is not treason, Anakin-it may be the only way to save the Republic!"»
- L4175 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"So all you're really asking," Anakin said slowly, "is for me to help the Council find Darth Sidious."»
- L4179 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Locked within the furnace of his heart, Anakin whispered an echo-not quite an echo-slightly altered, just at the end: I would ask him to sit down, and I would ask him if he has any power he could use--to save Padme.»
- L4189 [SAID ABOUT (by Yoda)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Know how important your friendship with young Anakin is to you, I do." Yoda, too, stared out toward the stark angles of the assault cruiser being loaded for the counterinvasion of Kashyyyk; he stood leaning on his gimer stick as though he did not trust his legs.»
- L4193 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «All he could remember was the look on Anakin's face.»
- L4215 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan accepted this with a nod, but still when he thought of Anakin, dread began to curdle below his heart. "I should have argued more strongly in Council today."»
- L4217 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"You think Skywalker won't be able to handle this?" Mace Windu said.»
- L4223 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"For Anakin," Obi-Wan said at length, "there is nothing more important than friendship.»
- L4229 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Any Jedi," Obi-Wan said, "except Anakin."»
- L4231 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan guessed they were remembering the times Anakin had violated orders-the times he had put at risk entire operations, the lives of thousands, the control of whole planetary systems-to save a friend.»
- L4243 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «I am firmly convinced that Anakin can do anything.»
- L4247 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan turned to stare once more toward the assault ship that would carry Yoda and the clone battalions to Kashyyyk, but he could see only Anakin's face.»

**Ch12 Not from a Jedi**

- L4263 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin barely noticed.»
- L4265 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «As Typho flew the vehicle off toward the immense residential tower's speeder park, she dismissed her two handmaidens and sent C-3PO on some busywork errand, then turned to lean on the veranda's balcony right where Anakin had leaned last night.»

- L4371 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, what is it?»
- L4377 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Don't shut me out, Anakin.»
- L4395 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Right now Anakin couldn't remember what that had been like.»

- L4625 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Obi-Wan," she said breathlessly, "has-" She bit off the following something happened to Anakin ? How would she explain why this was the first thing out of her mouth?»

- L5991 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Well, of course I hope you're correct, Anakin.»
- L6005 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «This is how it feels to be Anakin Skywalker, right now.»
- L6007 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «He lacks entirely the flash, the pure bold elan of an Anakin Skywalker; there is nowhere in him the penumbral ferocity of a Mace Windu or a Depa Billaba nor the stylish grace of a Shaak Ti or a Dooku, and he is nothing resembling the whirlwind of destruction that Yoda can become.»
- L6121 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «As his deactivated handgrip skittered and bounced along the tunnel behind him, he reflected that it was just as well Anakin wasn't there after all; he'd have never heard the end of it.»

**Ch16 Revelation**

- L6233 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"What about Skywalker?»
- L6253 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Skywalker?" Mace was at his side in an instant.»
- L6255 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin took Mace's arm in a grip of desperate strength, and used it like a crutch to haul himself upright.»
- L6261 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Steps-steps down-" Anakin's voice had a sharply bitter edge. "You have no idea ..."»
- L6263 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin-?»
- L6265 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Listen to me-you have to listen to me-" Anakin sagged against him, shaking; Mace wrapped his arms around the young Jedi and guided him into the nearest chair.»
- L6267 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Skywalker-Anakin.»
- L6269 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin collapsed forward, face into his hands.»
- L6273 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «The tangled web of fault lines in the Force he had seen connecting Anakin to Obi-Wan and to Palpatine was no more; in their place was a single spider-knot that sang with power enough to crack the planet.»
- L6273 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin Skywalker no longer had shatter-points.»
- L6275 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Mace said slowly, with the same sort of deliberate care he would use in examining an unknown type of bomb that might have the power to destroy the universe itself, "Anakin, look at me."»
- L6277 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Skywalker raised his head.»
- L6281 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin's eyes were raw, and red, and his face looked swollen.»
- L6281 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «For a long time he didn't know if Anakin would answer, if he could answer, if he could even speak at all; the young Jedi seemed to be struggling with something inside himself, as though he fought desperately against the birth of a monster hatching within his chest.»
- L6283 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «In the Force, Mace could feel the monster inside Anakin Skywalker, a real monster, too real, one that was eating him alive from the inside out.»
- L6285 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «This was the wound Anakin had taken.»
- L6287 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Finally, after what seemed forever, Anakin opened his blood-raw eyes.»
- L6295 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «What news could be bad enough to make a Jedi like Anakin Skywalker collapse?»
- L6295 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «What news could make Anakin Skywalker look like the stars had gone out?»
- L6297 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Then, in nine simple words, Anakin told him.»
- L6319 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «And because of that love now, here, in this instant, Anakin Skywalker has nine words for him that shred his heart, burn its pieces, and feed him its smoking ashes.»
- L6343 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, wait in the Council Chamber until we get back."»
- L6347 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"That's an order, Anakin."»
- L6349 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"But-but-but the Chancellor-" Anakin says desperately, clutching at the Jedi Master's hand.»

**Ch17 The Face of the Dark**

- L6407 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"And Skywalker?" The Zabrak Master cocked his head as though he felt a distant disturbance in the Force.»
- L6421 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Alone in the Chamber of the Jedi Council, Anakin Skywalker wrestled with his dragon.»
- L6447 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «instant he washer. . . he was the beat of her heart and he was the motion of her lips and he was her soft words as though she spoke a prayer to the stars-I love you, Anakin.»
- L6455 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «All things die, Anakin Skywalker.»
- L6463 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin stopped.»
- L6513 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin," she said warmly.»
- L6521 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"The Temple is sealed, Anakin.»
- L6529 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, why?»
- L6629 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin's speeder shrieked through the rain, dodging forked bolts of lightning that shot up from towers into the clouds, slicing across traffic lanes, screaming past spacescrapers so fast that his shock-wake cracked windows as he passed.»
- L6649 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin remembered him in the arena at Geonosis, effortlessly carving his way through wave after wave of combat droids, on his lips a gently humorous smile as though the horrific battle were only some friendly jest.»
- L6653 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin's own blade sang blue as it slashed through the window and he dived through the gap.»
- L6655 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin skidded to a stop.»
- L6673 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin blinked and rubbed his eyes again.»
- L6677 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Their blades flared and flashed, crashing together with bursts of fire, weaving nets of killing energy in exchanges so fast that Anakin could not truly see them-But he could feel them in the Force.»
- L6683 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Anakin could feel how the Force fed upon the shadow's murderous exaltation; he could feel fury spray into the Force though some poisonous abscess had crested in both their hearts.»
- L6733 [SAID ABOUT (by Palpatine / Darth Sidious)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Do you see, Anakin?»
- L6757 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin!" Mace called.»
- L6757 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, help me!»
- L6759 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «He felt Anakin's leap from the office floor to the ledge, felt his approach behind-And Palpatine was not afraid.»
- L6765 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Mace thought, blankly astonished, Palpatine trusts Anakin Skywalker . .»
- L6769 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Now Anakin was at Mace's shoulder.»
- L6769 [NARRATION] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Palpatine still made no move to defend himself from Skywalker; instead he ramped up the lightning bursting from his hands, bending the fountain of Mace's blade back toward the Korun Master's face.»
- L6771 [SAID ABOUT (by Palpatine / Darth Sidious)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"He is a traitor, Anakin.»
- L6773 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"You're the chosen one, Anakin," Mace said, his voice going thin with strain.»
- L6775 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Skywalker echoed him faintly. "Destiny ..."»
- L6777 [SAID ABOUT (by Palpatine / Darth Sidious)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"He is killing me, Anakin-!»
- L6779 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, he's too strong for me-"»
- L6789 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «" Wait-" Skywalker seized his lightsaber arm with desperate strength. "Don't kill him-you can't just kill him, Master-"»
- L6793 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"You came to arrest him. He has to stand trial-"»
- L6797 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"So are you going to kill all them, too? Like he said you would?"»
- L6801 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «Skywalker's face swept itself clean of emotion. "That was different-"»
- L6807 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Anakin Skywalker, Jedi Knight (Council seat, not Master), 19 BBY] «"I need him alive!" Skywalker shouted. "I need him to save Padme!"»
- L6813 [NARRATION] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «He had been so intent on Palpatine's shatterpoint that he'd never thought to look for Anakin's.»
- L6817 [NARRATION] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «Anakin Skywalker knelt in the rain.»
- L6829 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «"You're following your destiny, Anakin," said a familiar gentle voice.»
- L6831 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «"You were right," Anakin heard himself saying. "Why didn't I know?"»
- L6835 [NARRATION] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «Anakin stared at the hand, but he no longer saw it.»
- L6845 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «"I do not fear your power, Anakin, I embrace it.»
- L6845 [NARRATION] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «I believe that, Anakin.»
- L6847 [NARRATION] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «Anakin looked from the dead hand on the ledge to the living one on his shoulder, then up to the face of the man who stood above him, and what he saw there choked him like an invisible fist crushing his throat.»
- L6855 [NARRATION] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «Stunned with horror, stunned with revulsion, Anakin could only stare at the creature.»
- L6863 [NARRATION] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «Anakin stood just within the office.»
- L6865 [NARRATION] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «Anakin couldn't tell if his expression might be revulsion, or if this were merely the new shape of his features.»
- L6869 [NARRATION] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «A voluminous robe of heavy black-on-black brocade floated downward from it; Anakin felt the current in the Force that carried the robe to Palpatine's hand.»
- L6875 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, it's time.»
- L6877 [NARRATION] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «Anakin didn't respond.»
- L6881 [NARRATION] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «A wave of tingling started at the base of Anakin's skull and spread over his whole body in a slow-motion shockwave.»
- L6887 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «Anakin shook his head and found that the rest of him threatened to begin shaking as well. "I-came to save your life, sir. Not to betray my friends-"»
- L6891 [NARRATION] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «Anakin could find no answer.»
- L6893 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «"And do you think that task is finished, my boy?" Sidious seated himself on the corner of the desk, hands folded in his lap, the way he always had when offering Anakin fatherly advice; the misshapen mask of his face made the familiarity of his posture into something horrible.»
- L6895 [NARRATION] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «Anakin stared at his hands.»
- L6897 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «"It's them or me, Anakin.»
- L6899 [NARRATION] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «Anakin made his right hand-his black-gloved hand of durasteel and electrodrivers-into a fist.»
- L6905 [NARRATION] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «From a remote, frozen distance that was at the same time more extravagantly, hotly intimate than he could have ever dreamed, Anakin handled his emotions.»
- L6909 [NARRATION] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «Anakin opened his eyes and turned his gaze fully upon the grotesque features of Darth Sidious.»
- L6915 [NARRATION] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «Anakin lifted his hand of durasteel and electrodrivers and cupped it, staring into its palm as though he held there the fear that had haunted his dreams for his whole life, and it was no larger than the piece of shuura he'd once stolen from Padme's plate.»
- L6933 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «"The Jedi betrayed you," Anakin said. "The Jedi betrayed both of us."»
- L6941 [SAID ABOUT] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «"Kneel before me, Anakin Skywalker."»
- L6943 [NARRATION] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «Anakin dropped to one knee.»
- L6949 [NARRATION] [Anakin, mid-fall (Mace duel to Sith pledge), 19 BBY] «Darth Sidious laid a pale hand on Anakin's brow.»
- L6953 [NARRATION] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «Vader.»
- L6955 [NARRATION] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «Vader, he said to himself.»
- L6955 [NARRATION] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «Vader.»
- L6963 [SAID ABOUT] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «Do what must be done, Lord Vader."»
- L6973 [SAID ABOUT] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «"Rise, Darth Vader."»
- L6975 [NARRATION] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «The Sith Lord who once had been a Jedi hero called Anakin Skywalker stood, drawing himself up to his full height, but he looked not outward upon his new Master, nor upon the planet-city beyond, nor out into the galaxy that they would soon rule.»
- L6977 [NARRATION] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «I am Darth Vader, he said within himself.»
- L6981 [NARRATION] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «I am Darth Vader, he repeated as he ground the dragon's corpse to dust beneath his mental heel, as he watched the dragon's dust and ashes scatter before the blast from his furnace heart, and you-You are nothing at all.»
- L6987 [NARRATION] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «Anakin Skywalker.»
- L6991 [NARRATION] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «Anakin stood in the night outside, shoulders hunched, head down against the rain.»
- L6993 [SAID ABOUT] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «"Anakin!" he gasped, running up to the young man.»
- L6993 [SAID ABOUT] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, what happened?»
- L6995 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «Anakin looked at him as though he wasn't sure who the Gate Master was. "Where is Shaak Ti?"»

She's searching the Force in deep meditation, trying to get some feel for what's going on . . ." His words trailed away. Anakin didn't seem to be listening. "Something has happened, hasn't it?"»
- L7001 [SAID ABOUT] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «"Anakin," he said slowly, "what's going on?»
- L7003 [NARRATION] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «The last thing Jurokk felt was the emitter of a lightsaber against the soft flesh beneath his jaw; the last thing he heard as blue plasma chewed upward through his head and burst from the top of his skull and burned away his life, was Anakin Skywalker's melancholy reply.»

- L7301 [SAID ABOUT] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «"Anakin-"»
- L7331 [SAID ABOUT] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «"Always, Anakin.»
- L7359 [NARRATION] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «C-3PO barely had time to wish his little friend good luck and remind him to stay alert as Master Anakin brushed past him and climbed into the starfighter's cockpit, then fired the engine and blasted off, taking R2-D2 goodness knows where-probably to some preposterously horrible alien»

- L7511 [NARRATION] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «Anakin must have fallen along with Mace and Agen, Saesee and Kit; fallen along with the Temple.»
- L7563 [NARRATION] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «The loss of Anakin stabbed him.»

**Ch19 The Face of the Sith**

- L7617 [SAID ABOUT] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «Has my new apprentice, Darth Vader, arrived?"»
- L7627 [SAID ABOUT] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «"Welcome, Lord Vader!" His elongated legs almost tangled with each other in his rush to shake the hand of the Sith Lord.»

- L7929 [NARRATION] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «Consumed by Darth Vader.»

**Ch20 Chiaroscuro**

- L7951 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «As C-3PO swiftly ushered him into the sitting room, Master Kenobi asked, "Has Anakin been here?"»
- L7969 [NARRATION] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «There followed some discussion that C-3PO didn't entirely understand; it was political information entirely outside his programming, having to do with Master Anakin, and the Republic having fallen, whatever that meant, and with something called a Sith Lord, and Chancellor Palpatine, and the dark side of the Force, and really, he couldn't make»
- L7969 [NARRATION] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «The only parts he clearly understood had to do with the Jedi Order being outlawed and all but wiped out (that news had been all over the Lipartian Way this morning) and the not-altogether-unexpected revelation that Master Kenobi had come here seeking Master Anakin.»
- L7969 [NARRATION] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «They were partners, after all (though despite all their years together, Master Anakin's recent behavior made it sadly clear that Master Kenobi's lovely manners had entirely failed to rub off).»
- L7975 [NARRATION] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «Three years running the household of a career politician stopped C-3PO from popping back out and reminding the Senator that Master Anakin had told her just yesterday he was on his way to Mustafar; he knew very well that the Senator's memory failed only when she decided it should.»
- L7977 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «"Anakin must be found.»
- L7989 [SAID ABOUT] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «"They were murdered by Anakin.''»
- L7991 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «"He could never . . . he could never . . . not my Anakin .»
- L8007 [SAID ABOUT] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «"Anakin is the father, isn't he?"»
- L8047 [NARRATION] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «Darth Vader stood on the command bridge of the Mustafar control center, hand of durasteel clasping hand of flesh behind him, and gazed up through the transparisteel view wall at the galaxy he would one day rule.»
- L8065 [NARRATION] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «He was Darth Vader.»
- L8125 [NARRATION] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «A silvery flash outside caught Darth Vader's eye, as though an elegantly curved mirror swung through the smoke and cinders, picking up the shine of white-hot lava.»
- L8131 [SAID ABOUT] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «"You have restored peace and justice to the galaxy, Lord Vader."»

- L8143 [NARRATION] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «He spent a moment reassembling his Anakin Skywalker face: he let Anakin Sky-walker's love flow through him, let Anakin Skywalker's glad smile come to his lips, let Anakin Skywalker's youthful energy bring a joyous bounce to his step as he trotted to the entrance over the mess of corpses and severed body parts.»

- L8195 [SAID ABOUT] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, my Anakin ..." She shivered against his chest.»
- L8199 [SAID ABOUT] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «"It wasn't that, my love, it was-oh, Anakin, he said such terrible things about you!"»
- L8219 [SAID ABOUT] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «"No, Anakin!»
- L8223 [SAID ABOUT] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, what-"»

- L8327 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Darth Vader, newly turned (unsuited, Mustafar-era), 19 BBY] «In the Force, Anakin burned like a fusion torch. "You turned her against me."»

- L271 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «This is Obi-Wan Kenobi:»
- L279 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan feels the truth of this every day.»
- L285 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «And Obi-Wan Kenobi knows, too, that to have lived his life without being Master to Anakin Skywalker would have left him a different man.»
- L289 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan sees so much of Qui-Gon in Anakin that sometimes it hurts his heart; at the very least, Anakin mirrors Qui-Gon's flair for the dramatic, and his casual disregard for rules.»

- L291 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan Kenobi has learned to relax.»
- L295 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «It is characteristic of Obi-Wan that he is entirely unaware of this.»
- L309 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Keep on it." He glanced at Obi-Wan's starfighter limping through the battle, a hundred meters off his left wing. "I can feel his jitters from all the way over here."»
- L339 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan's voice came over the cockpit speakers, flat and tight. "Does your droid have anything? Arfour's hopeless. I think that last cannon hit cooked his motivator."»
- L343 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"No." Obi-Wan sounded absolutely certain. "There's no need to consider it. Until the possible becomes actual, it is only a distraction. Be mindful of what is, not what might be."»

- L347 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, he was defended by Stass Allie and Shaak Ti. If two Masters could not prevent this, do you think you could? - L351 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Master Ti had faced him before, Anakin. After Muunilinst. She is not only subtle and experienced, but very capable indeed. Seats on the Jedi Council aren't handed out as party favors."»
- L373 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Oh. That one." He could practically hear Obi-Wan's stomach dropping. "Oh, this should be easy . . ."»
- L377 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Easy? No. But it might be fun." Sometimes a little teasing was the only way to get Obi-Wan to loosen up. "Lunch at Dex's says I'll blast two for each of yours. Artoo can keep score."»

- L393 [SAID ABOUT] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Anakin spun his comm dial to the new frequency in time to hear Obi-Wan say, "Oddball, do you copy?»

- L457 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Not long after he became Obi-Wan's Padawan, all those years ago, a minor mission had brought them to a dead system: one so immeasurably old that its star had long ago turned to a frigid dwarf of hypercompacted trace metals, hovering a quantum fraction of a degree above absolute zero.»

- L477 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «And the only answers he ever has for these dead cold whispers are his memories of Obi-Wan's voice, or Yoda's.»

- L675 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Even flying blind and nearly out of control through the middle of a space battle, Obi-Wan could not avoid a second of disbelief at the bewildering variety of auxiliary tools and aftermarket behaviors Anakin had tinkered onto his starfighter's astromech, even beyond the sophisticated upgrades performed by the Royal Engineers of Naboo.»

- L789 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan himself, beard rimed with frost, lightsaber out and flaming, stood in a tightening ring of battle droids.»

- L805 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Anakin whirled to find Obi-Wan right behind him in the act of slicing neatly through the braincase of a battle droid.»
- L809 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «This, then, is Obi-Wan and Anakin:»
- L811 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Though Obi-Wan is sixteen standard years Anakin's elder, they have become men together.»
- L813 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «The war that has done this is not the Clone Wars; Obi-Wan and Anakin's war began on Naboo, when Qui-Gon Jinn died at the hand of a Sith Lord.»
- L817 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Say what you will about the wisdom of ancient Master Yoda, or the deadly skill of grim Mace Windu, the courage of Ki-Adi-Mundi, or the subtle wiles of Shaak Ti; the greatness of all these Jedi is unquestioned, but it pales next to the legend that has grown around Kenobi and Skywalker.»
- L823 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan and Anakin always answer.»
- L825 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Whether Obi-Wan's legendary cleverness might beat Anakin's raw power, straight up, no rules, is the subject of schoolyard fist-fights, creche-pool wriggle-matches, and pod-chamber stinkwars across the Republic.»
- L827 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Anakin and Obi-Wan would never fight each other.»

**Ch2 Dooku**

- L963 [SAID ABOUT] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Lord Tyranus, Kenobi and Skywalker have arrived."»
- L995 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «The blue-scanned image before him now became miniatures of Kenobi and Skywalker as he had seen them so many times before: shoulder-to-shoulder, lightsabers whirling as they enthusiastically dismantled droid after droid after droid.»
- L1063 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Count Dooku watched with clinical distaste as the blue-scanned images of Kenobi and Skywalker engaged in a preposterous farce-chase, pursued by destroyer droids into and out of turbolift pods that shot upward and downward and even sideways.»

- L1107 [SAID ABOUT] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"May I suggest, Master, that we give Kenobi one last chance?»
- L1109 [SAID ABOUT] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Kenobi." His Master's voice went silken.»
- L1109 [SAID ABOUT] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"You have long been interested in Kenobi, haven't you?"»
- L1117 [SAID ABOUT] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Kenobi must die.»
- L1119 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «As long as Kenobi was alive, Skywalker would never be securely in the camp of the Sith; Kenobi's unshakable faith in the values of the Jedi would keep the Jedi blindfold on Skywalker's eyes and the Jedi shackles on the young man's true power.»
- L1143 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Improving upon his Master's plan was near to impossible; his own idea, of substituting Kenobi for Skywalker, he had to admit was only the product of a certain misplaced sentimentality.»

- L1173 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «The death of Obi-Wan Kenobi would be the death of the Republic.»
- L1181 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Kenobi and Skywalker are nearly at the door.»

**Ch3 The Way Of The Sith**

- L3495 [SAID ABOUT (by General Grievous)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Kenobi?" Grievous's fists clenched hard enough that his carpal electrodrivers whined in protest.»
- L3499 [SAID ABOUT (by General Grievous)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Kenobi will die."»
- L3521 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «And coming toward him from the direction of the shuttle, shielding his eyes and leaning against the morning wind that whipped across the unprotected field-was that Obi-Wan?»
- L3523 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «He'd scoured the Temple for his former Master; he'd nearly giving up hope of finding him when a passing Padawan had mentioned that he'd seen Obi-Wan on his way out to the landing deck to meet Palpatine's shuttle.»
- L3523 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «He hoped Obi-Wan wouldn't notice he hadn't changed his clothes.»
- L3563 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Let him think he was talking about Obi-Wan.»
- L3583 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «And then go find Obi-Wan.»
- L3597 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «But now here was Obi-Wan-Obi-Wan would help him, Anakin knew he would-if only Anakin could figure out the right way to ask . . .»
- L3599 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «While he was still hunting for words, Obi-Wan reached him. "You missed the report on the Outer Rim sieges."»
- L3607 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Quite the opposite," Obi-Wan said. "That shuttle did not bring the Chancellor. It is waiting to bring you to him."»
- L3611 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"The Council," Obi-Wan said, "has not been consulted."»
- L3615 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Nor do I." Obi-Wan stepped close, nodding minutely back toward the shuttle. "They simply arrived, some time ago. When the deck-duty Padawans questioned them, they said the Chancellor has requested your presence."»
- L3619 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Perhaps he has some reason to believe," Obi-Wan said carefully, "that the Council might have resisted sending you. Perhaps he did not wish to reveal his reason for this summons. Relations between the Council and the Chancellor are . . . stressed."»
- L3621 [SAID ABOUT (by Anakin Skywalker / Darth Vader)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Obi-Wan, what's going on?»
- L3623 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Know? No: only suspect. Which is not at all the same thing."»
- L3627 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"And that's why I am out here, Anakin. So I can talk to you. Privately. Not as a member of the Jedi Council-in fact, if the Council were to find out about this conversation . . . well, let's say, I'd rather they didn't."»
- L3631 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"None of us does. Not really." Obi-Wan put a hand on Anakin's shoulder and frowned deeply into his eyes. "Anakin, you know I am your friend."»
- L3635 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"No. No of courses, Anakin. Nothing is of course anymore. I am your friend, and as your friend, I am asking you: be wary of Palpatine."»
- L3639 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"I know you are his friend. I am concerned that he may not be yours. Be careful of him, Anakin. And be careful of your own feelings."»
- L3643 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan's frown deepened. "No. I don't. The Force grows ever darker around us, and we are all affected by it, even as we affect it. This is a dangerous time to be a Jedi. Please, Anakin-please be careful"»
- L3647 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"I have to-"»
- L3651 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan's frown softened toward a smile. "How did you know I was going to say that?"»
- L3657 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «the long comfortable couches, the huge arc of window behind Palpatine's desk-these were all so familiar that they were usually almost invisible, but today-Today, with Obi-Wan's voice whispering be wary of Palpatine in the back of his head, everything looked different.»
- L3691 [SAID ABOUT] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «And, I suppose, wiser." He didn't want to get into this with Palpatine; talking with the Chancellor like this-seriously, man-to-man-made him feel good, feel strong, despite Obi-Wan's warning.»
- L3707 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"With respect, sir, many on the Council would say the same of you." He thought of Obi-Wan, and he had to stop himself from wincing. Had he betrayed a confidence just now?»
- L3709 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Or had Obi-Wan been doing the Council's bidding after all? . . .»
- L3769 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Inside his head, he heard the echo of Obi-Wan, murmuring relations between the Council and the Chancellor are . . . stressed.»
- L3787 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «He might sit next to Ki-Adi-Mundi, or Plo Koon-or even beside Obi-Wan!»

**Ch11 Politics**

- L3813 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «the last reminders of the fires of days past; chill remnants of night slid down the High Council Tower of the Jedi Temple; and within the cloistered chamber itself, Obi-Wan was still trying to talk them out of it.»
- L3817 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «But Obi-Wan knew of this tradition only by reputation, from tales in the archives and stories told by Masters whose tenure on the Council predated the return of the Sith.»
- L3817 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «In the all-too-short years since Obi-Wan's own elevation, argument in this Chamber was more the rule than the exception.»

We have seen that young Skywalker has the power to battle a Sith Lord alone, if need be; he has»
- L3847 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan felt the ripple of agreement flow through the Chamber, and Yoda nodded solemnly.»
- L3855 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"This sounds like a good plan," Obi-Wan said. "But what Master do you have in mind?"»

- L3919 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan sat in the chair that once had belonged to Oppo Rancisis, looking pensive.»

Go ahead and Vaapad this, you-"Anakin," Obi-Wan said softly. He gestured to an empty seat beside him. "Please."»
- L3965 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «And something in Obi-Wan's gentle voice, in his simple, straightforward request, sent his anger slinking off ashamed, and Anakin found himself alone on the carpet in the middle of the Jedi Council, blinking.»

- L3981 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «And as the holopresences of Plo Koon and Ki-Adi-Mundi winked out, as Obi-Wan and Agen Kolar rose and spoke together in tones softly grave, as Yoda and Mace Windu walked from the room, Anakin could only sit, sick at heart, stunned with helplessness.»
- L4043 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Anakin was glad the vast vaulted Temple hallway was deserted save for him and Obi-Wan; he didn't have to keep his voice down.»

- L4067 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"No one cares about that-"»
- L4071 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"This has nothing to do with Master Yoda."»
- L4075 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Yes, Anakin, yes. Of course. No one questions your accomplishments. It's your relationship to Palpatine that is the problem. And it is a very serious problem."»
- L4079 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, stop. Listen to yourself. Your thoughts are of jealousy, and pride. These are dark thoughts, Anakin. How can you be a Jedi Master when you have not»
- L4083 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan frowned. "I'm sorry?"»
- L4087 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"The Council does not operate that way, Anakin, and you know it."»
- L4093 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan's eyes shifted, and the sick fatigue in Anakin's guts turned darker.»
- L4093 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «How bad did it have to be to make Obi-Wan unable to look him in the eye?»
- L4095 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Anakin, look, I'm on your side," Obi-Wan said softly. He looked tired, too: he looked as tired and sick as Anakin felt. "I never wanted to see you put in this situation."»
- L4097 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"What situation?" Still Obi-Wan hesitated.»
- L4099 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Come on, Obi-Wan.»
- L4101 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan glanced around the empty hall as if he wanted to make sure they were still alone; Anakin had a feeling it was just an excuse to avoid facing him when he spoke.»
- L4103 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"The Council," Obi-Wan said slowly, "approved your appointment because Palpatine trusts you. They want you to report on all his dealings. They have to know what he's up to."»
- L4105 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «No wonder Obi-Wan couldn't look him in the face.»
- L4105 [SAID ABOUT] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Obi-Wan, that's treason!"»
- L4107 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"We are at war, Anakin." Obi-Wan looked thoroughly miserable. "The Council is sworn to uphold the principles of the Republic through any means necessary. We have to. Especially when the greatest enemy of those principles seems to be the Chancellor himself!"»
- L4111 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Because it's not for the record, Anakin. You must be able to understand why."»
- L4115 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"It isn't," Obi-Wan insisted. He looked wounded. "It's about keeping an eye on who he deals with, and who deals with him."»
- L4117 [SAID ABOUT (by Anakin Skywalker / Darth Vader)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"He's not a bad man, Obi-Wan-he's a great man, who's holding this Republic together with his bare hands-"»
- L4119 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"By staying in office long after his term has expired. By gathering dictatorial powers-"»
- L4123 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Don't be naive. The Senate is so intimidated they give him anything he wants!"»
- L4127 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"That is what we're asking you to do, Anakin."»
- L4133 [SAID ABOUT] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Finally, he said, "He's my friend, Obi-Wan."»
- L4135 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Yes," Obi-Wan said softly. Sadly. "I know."»
- L4139 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Now it was Obi-Wan's turn to fall silent.»
- L4157 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan stepped closer and put a hand on Anakin's arm.»

- L4177 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Yes." Obi-Wan looked relieved, incredibly relieved, as though some horrible chronic pain had suddenly and inexplicably eased. "Yes, that's it exactly."»
- L4183 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan stared past Yoda and Mace Windu, out through the gunship's window at the vast deployment platform and the swarm of clones who were loading the assault cruiser at the far end.»
- L4191 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Another man-even another Jedi-might have resented the rebuke, but Obi-Wan only sighed. "I suppose-he is the chosen one, after all. The prophecy says he was born to bring balance to the Force, but . . ."»
- L4205 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"So you both feel it, too," Obi-Wan said. The words hurt him. "You both can feel that we have turned some invisible corner."»
- L4211 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"And what if the light just isn't there?" Obi-Wan asked. "What if we get to the end of this tunnel and find only night?"»
- L4215 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan accepted this with a nod, but still when he thought of Anakin, dread began to curdle below his heart.»
- L4219 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"I trust him with my life," Obi-Wan said simply. "And that is precisely the problem."»

- L4227 [SAID ABOUT (by Yoda)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"As I would yours, young Obi-Wan.»
- L4229 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Any Jedi," Obi-Wan said, "except Anakin."»
- L4231 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan guessed they were remembering the times Anakin had violated orders-the times he had put at risk entire operations, the lives of thousands, the control of whole planetary systems-to save a friend.»
- L4233 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «More than once, in fact, to save Obi-Wan.»

**Ch17 The Face of the Dark**

- L6397 [SAID ABOUT] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Or even Kenobi.»

- L7335 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Icy salt water shocked Obi-Wan back to full consciousness.»
- L7339 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan was fairly certain that his current situation qualified as an emergency.»
- L7345 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Using her massive body to shield Obi-Wan from his own troops.»
- L7347 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Boga had known, somehow . . . the dragonmount had known what Obi-Wan had been incapable of even suspecting, and without hesitation she'd given her life to save her rider.»

- L7351 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «He let grief take him for a moment; grief not for the death of a noble beast, but for how little time Obi-Wan had had to appreciate the gift of his friend's service.»

- L7409 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «Reigniting his lightsaber and moving cautiously back out into the cavern, Obi-Wan came upon a nest of what must have been infant Huge Slimy Cave-Monsters; picking his way around it as they lunged and snapped and squalled at him, he reflected absently that people who thought all babies were cute should really get out more.»

- L7483 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY] «"Please repeat," Obi-Wan said. "I'm locking onto your signal. Please repeat."»

- L8587 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «He might have left Obi-Wan alone: the last Jedi.»

- L8593 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «In the end, he was still Obi-Wan Kenobi, and he was still a Jedi, and he would not murder a helpless man.»

- L8603 [SAID ABOUT] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «"Already inside, sir, but she is badly hurt." Obi-Wan ran up the ramp to the skiff's cockpit and fired the engines.»

**Ch21 A New Jedi Order**

- L8673 [SAID ABOUT] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «"Obi-Wan is asking for you at the surgical theater," he said "It's Padme.»
- L8675 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan sat beside her, holding one cold, still hand in both of his. "Don't give up, Padme."»
- L8685 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan cast an appeal toward the medical droid. "Can't you do something?"»
- L8689 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «Not physically, Obi-Wan thought. He squeezed her hand as though he could keep life within her body by simple pressure. "Padme, you have to hold on."»
- L8705 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «"Padme, you have twins," Obi-Wan said desperately. "They need you-please hang on . . ."»
- L8713 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «In the Force, Obi-Wan felt Yoda's approach, and he looked up to see the ancient Master beside Bail Organa, both staring the same grave question down through the surgical theater's observation panel.»
- L8715 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «The only answer Obi-Wan had was a helpless shake of his head.»
- L8717 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «Padme reached across with her free hand, with the hand she had laid upon the brow of her firstborn son, and pressed something into Obi-Wan's palm.»
- L8721 [SAID ABOUT] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «"Obi-Wan . . . there ... is still good in him.»
- L8733 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan looked down at the simple, child-like symbols carved into it, and felt from it in the Force soaring echoes of transcendent love, and the bleak, black despair of unendurable heartbreak.»
- L8737 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «Around a conference table on Tantive IV, Bail Organa, Obi-Wan Kenobi, and Yoda met to decide the fate of the galaxy.»
- L8741 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «"We should split them up," Obi-Wan said. "Even if the Sith find one, the other may survive. I can take the boy, Master Yoda, and you take the girl. We can hide them away, keep them safe-train them as Anakin should have been trained-"»
- L8745 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan looked uncertain. "But how are they to learn the self-discipline a Jedi needs? How are they to master skills of the Force?"»

- L8751 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «Yoda and Obi-Wan exchanged a look.»
- L8753 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «Bail stood, a little jerkily, as though he simply could no longer keep his seat. - L8759 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «"Anakin survived it," Obi-Wan said. "Luke can, too. And I can-well, I could take him there, and watch over him. Protect him from the worst of the planet's dangers, until he can learn to protect himself."»

- L8763 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «"More an ... eccentric old uncle, I think. It is a part I can play very well. To keep watch over Anakin's son-" Obi-Wan sighed, finally allowing his face to register a suggestion of his old gentle smile. "I can't imagine a better way to spend the rest of my life."»
- L8767 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «"Strong the Force runs, in the Skywalker line. Only hope, we can. Until the time is right, disappear we will."»
- L8771 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «"Trust in this, we always will. Go now; for happy news, your Queen is waiting."»
- L8775 [NARRATION] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «When Obi-Wan moved to follow, Yoda's gimer stick barred his way.»
- L8775 [SAID ABOUT] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «"A moment, Master Kenobi.»
- L8777 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan blinked. "Your new Master?"»
- L8781 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Obi-Wan Kenobi (Mustafar duel and after), 19 BBY] «C-3PO shuffled along the starship's hallway beside R2-D2, following Senator Organa who had, by all accounts, inherited them both.
