### 9.3 Palpatine / Darth Sidious (601 quotes: 152 own lines, 156 said-about, 293 narration)

**Introduction: The Age of Heroes**

- L57 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «The nightmare gets worse: the Supreme Chancellor is missing.»
- L59 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Palpatine of Naboo, the most admired man in the galaxy, whose unmatched political skills have held the Republic together.»
- L61 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Palpatine is more than respected.»
- L63 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Every one of them knows it in her heart, in his gut, in its very bones-Without Palpatine, the Republic will fall.»
- L65 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Supreme Chancellor Palpatine has been captured by the Separatists-and not just the Separatists.»
- L69 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Nute Gunray is treacherous and venal, but he's Neimoidian: venality and treachery are expected, and in the Chancellor of the Trade Federation they're even virtues.»
- L79 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «And his durasteel grip has closed upon Palpatine.»
- L105 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Not on the HoloNet, of course-the HoloNet news is under the control of the Office of the Supreme Chancellor, and not even Palpatine's renowned candor would allow tales like these to be told-but people hear whispers.»
- L111 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Palpatine is captured.»

**Ch1 Anakin And Obi-Wan**

- L341 [SAID ABOUT (by Anakin Skywalker / Darth Vader)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Have you thought about how we'll find the Chancellor if-"»
- L355 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «If he had been here, Chancellor Palpatine would be home and safe already.»
- L355 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Instead, Anakin had been stuck running around the Outer Rim for months like some useless Padawan, and all Palpatine had for protectors were Jedi who were clever and subtle.»
- L371 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «The vulture fighters clinging to the long curves of the Trade Federation cruiser indicated by Palpatine's beacon gave it eerily life-like ripples, like some metallic marine predator bristling with Alderaanian walking barnacles.»
- L411 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «With Palpatine's life at stake?»
- L487 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «The Supreme Chancellor has been family to Anakin: always there, always caring, always free with advice and unstinting aid.»
- L487 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «He can tell Palpatine things he could never share with his Master.»
- L489 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «He can tell Palpatine things he can't even tell Padme.»
- L491 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Now the Supreme Chancellor is in the worst kind of danger.»
- L495 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «If anyone can save Palpatine, Anakin will.»
- L579 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Every one of them would gladly trade his life for Palpatine's.»
- L579 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Will you trade Palpatine's life for theirs?"»
- L631 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Get the Chancellor!."»
- L791 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «of a starfighter in the Theed royal hangar, his first touch of a real ship's real cannons blasting battle droids-He'd have done the same right here, except that Palpatine was somewhere on this ship.»
- L791 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «They just might need one of the light shuttles in this hangar to get the Chancellor safely to the surface; a few dozen cannon blasts bouncing around in here could wreck them all.»
- L797 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Find Palpatine.»

**Ch2 Dooku**

- L851 [SAID ABOUT (by Anakin Skywalker / Darth Vader)] [Sidious (hologram to Dooku), 19 BBY] «"Artoo, where's the Chancellor?"»
- L853 [NARRATION] [Sidious (hologram to Dooku), 19 BBY] «Its holoprojector eye swiveled and the blue scanning laser built a ghostly image near Anakin's boot: Palpatine shackled into a large swivel chair.»
- L857 [NARRATION] [Sidious (hologram to Dooku), 19 BBY] «Palpatine looked as if he'd aged ten years since Anakin had last seen him.»
- L857 [NARRATION] [Sidious (hologram to Dooku), 19 BBY] «If Grievous had hurt the Chancellor-had so much as touched him-The hand of jointed durasteel inside his black glove clenched so hard that electronic feedback made his shoulder ache.»
- L869 [NARRATION] [Sidious (hologram to Dooku), 19 BBY] «Palpatine appeared to be alone: the chair sat in the center of an arc of empty floor, facing a huge curved viewing wall.»
- L899 [NARRATION] [Sidious (hologram to Dooku), 19 BBY] «Do you think Grievous couldn't have found Palpatine's beacon?»
- L899 [NARRATION] [Sidious (hologram to Dooku), 19 BBY] «It can hardly be accident that through all the ECM, the Chancellor's homing signal was in the clear.»
- L971 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious (hologram to Dooku), 19 BBY] «"But driving them to you also sends them directly toward the Chancellor himself.»
- L973 [SAID ABOUT (by Count Dooku / Darth Tyranus)] [Sidious (hologram to Dooku), 19 BBY] «"Matters are so," Count Dooku said, "because Lord Sidious wishes them so; should you desire to press your objections, please feel at liberty to take them up with him."»
- L1017 [NARRATION] [Sidious (hologram to Dooku), 19 BBY] «He is to the Confederacy of Independent Systems what Palpatine is to the Republic: the living symbol of the justice of its cause.»
- L1121 [NARRATION] [Sidious (hologram to Dooku), 19 BBY] «This had all come about too quickly; had Sidious thought through all the implications of this operation?»
- L1141 [NARRATION] [Sidious (hologram to Dooku), 19 BBY] «Not only had the Dark Lord introduced Dooku to realms of power beyond his most spectacular fantasies, but Sidious was also a political manipulator so subtle that his abilities might be considered to dwarf even the power of the dark side itself.»
- L1145 [NARRATION] [Sidious (hologram to Dooku), 19 BBY] «He should be; Darth Sidious had spent a considerable number of years making him so.»
- L1149 [NARRATION] [Sidious (hologram to Dooku), 19 BBY] «Dooku understood that this was more than a test for Skywalker; though Sidious had never said so directly, Dooku was certain that he himself was being tested as well.»
- L1149 [NARRATION] [Sidious (hologram to Dooku), 19 BBY] «Success today would show his Master that he was worthy of the mantle of Mastery himself: by the end of the coming battle, he would have initiated Skywalker into the manifold glories of the dark side, just as Sidious had initiated him.»
- L1157 [NARRATION] [Sidious (hologram to Dooku), 19 BBY] «For the first time since Sidious had revealed the true subtlety of this masterpiece, Dooku allowed himself to relax enough to imagine the outcome.»
- L1185 [NARRATION] [Sidious (hologram to Dooku), 19 BBY] «Darth Sidious, Dark Lord of the Sith, sat in the General's Chair, shackled to it at the wrist and ankle.»
- L1189 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Sidious (hologram to Dooku), 19 BBY] «Palpatine of Naboo, Supreme Chancellor of the Republic, replied, "Withdraw. They are here."»

**Ch3 The Way Of The Sith**

- L1207 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Once the Chancellor is safe," Obi-Wan said with a ghost of a smile, "we can blow up the ship."»
- L1215 [SAID ABOUT (by Anakin Skywalker / Darth Vader)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Or Chancellor Palpatine."»
- L1251 [SAID ABOUT (by Anakin Skywalker / Darth Vader)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Obi-Wan," Anakin said, "let's go get the Chancellor."»
- L1259 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Dooku nodded judiciously to himself, frowning down at the translucent blue ghosts slinking toward Palpatine.»
- L1285 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «The dark figure in the chair-it was Chancellor Palpatine, it was, and there were no droids to be seen, and his heart should have leapt within his chest, but-Palpatine looked bad.»
- L1287 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «And worse-Anakin saw in the Chancellor's face something he'd never dreamed he'd find there, and it squeezed breath from his lungs and wiped words from his brain.»
- L1289 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Palpatine looked frightened.»
- L1297 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Palpatine's only response was a tight murmur. "Anakin, behind you-!"»
- L1301 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «In the Force, he could feel the focus of Palpatine's eyes: the source of the fear that rolled off him in billows like vapor down a block of frozen air.»
- L1309 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"This," he murmured to Palpatine, and to himself, "is not a problem."»
- L1325 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «At least Palpatine was here, shackled within the great chair at the far end of the room, the space battle whirling upon the view wall behind him as though his stark silhouette spread great wings of war.»
- L1325 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «But Palpatine was less audience than he was author.»
- L1333 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Dooku derived a certain melancholy satisfaction-a pleasurably lonely contemplation of his own unrecognized greatness-from a brief reflection that Skywalker would never understand how much thought and planning, how much work, Lord Sidious had invested in so hastily orchestrating his sham victory.»
- L1341 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «This was the real power of the dark side, the power he had suspected even as a boy, had sought through his long life until Darth Sidious had shown him that it had been his all along.»
- L1351 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «And then there was Palpatine, of course: he was beyond power.»
- L1351 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Though seen with the eyes of the dark side itself, Palpatine was an event horizon.»
- L1359 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «His reply was clearly intended as much for Dooku as for Palpatine.»
- L1371 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Let's not make a mess of this in front of the Chancellor."»
- L1375 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «I could have taken the Chancellor outsystem hours ago.»
- L1381 [SAID ABOUT (by Count Dooku / Darth Tyranus)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Little danger to the three of us, of course, but I should certainly hate for any harm to come to the Chancellor."»
- L1391 [SAID ABOUT (by Count Dooku / Darth Tyranus)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"I bear Chancellor Palpatine no ill will, foolish boy.»
- L1405 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"We're on to your partner Sidious; we tracked him all over the galaxy.»
- L1407 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «He was terribly, terribly tempted to wink at Palpatine, but of course that would never do.»
- L1411 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Lord Sidious would take it from there.»
- L1443 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Sidious will have to help him with that.»
- L1505 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Lord Sidious could come up with a new plan more easily than a new apprentice.»
- L1551 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «He understood why Sidious had been so interested in him for so long.»
- L1571 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Then Sidious, for some reason, decided to intervene.»
- L1573 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Don't fear what you're feeling, Anakin, use it!" he barked in Palpatine's voice. "Call upon your fury. Focus it, and he cannot stand against you. Rage is your weapon. Strike now! Strike! Kill him!"»
- L1577 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «He and Skywalker paused for one single, final instant, blades locked together, staring at each other past a sizzling cross of scarlet against blue, and in that instant Dooku found himself wondering in bewildered astonishment if Sidious had suddenly lost his mind.»

**Ch4 Jedi Trap**

- L1609 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «hand, how Dooku could strike down even Obi-Wan himself seemingly without effort and now Anakin is all alone and he will never be a match for any Lord of the Sith-But Palpatine's words rage is your weapon have given Anakin permission to unseal the shielding around his furnace heart, and all his fears and all his doubts shrivel in its flame.»
- L1635 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Dooku, cringing, shrinking with dread, still finds some hope in his heart that he is wrong, that Palpatine has not betrayed him, that this has all been proceeding according to plan-Until he hears "Good, Anakin!»
- L1635 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «I knew you could do it!" and registers this is Palpatine's voice and feels within the darkest depths of all he is the approach of the words that are to come next.»
- L1637 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Kill him," Palpatine says. "Kill him now."»
- L1645 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"A deal only if you released me," Palpatine replies, cold as intergalactic space. "Not if you used me as bait to kill my friends."»
- L1647 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Sidious's plan, not his own.»
- L1651 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Anakin," Palpatine says quietly. "Finish him."»
- L1657 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «But when Palpatine barks, "Do it!»
- L1713 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"You did well, Anakin." Palpatine's voice was warm as an arm around Anakin's shoulders.»
- L1715 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «From the Chancellor this sounded true, but when Anakin repeated it inside his head he knew that Palpatine's truth would be one he could never make himself believe.»
- L1739 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Have you never noticed that the Jedi way," Palpatine said, invisible now within the stark shadow of the General's Chair, "is not always the right way?"»
- L1741 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Anakin looked toward the shadow.»
- L1761 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"And we won't. Just as we need never speak of what has happened here today." It was as though the shadow itself spoke kindly. "I have always kept your secrets, have I not?"»
- L1769 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Anakin, my restraints, please," the shadow said. "I'm afraid this ship is breaking up. I don't think we should be aboard when it does."»
- L1771 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «In the Force, the field-signatures of the magnetic locks on the Chancellor's shackles were as clear as text saying unlock me like this; a simple twist of Anakin's mind popped them open.»
- L1771 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «The shadow grew a head, then shoulders, then underwent a sudden mitosis that left the General's Chair standing behind and turned its other half into the Supreme Chancellor.»
- L1773 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Palpatine picked his way through the debris that littered the gloom-shrouded room, moving surprisingly quickly toward the stairs. "Come along, Anakin. There is very little time."»
- L1775 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «The view wall flared white with the missiles' impacts, and one of them must have damaged the gravity generators: the ship seemed to heel over, forcing Palpatine to clutch desperately at the banister and sending Anakin skidding down a floor that had suddenly become a forty-five-degree ramp.»
- L1785 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Leave him, Anakin. There is no time." Palpatine was half hanging from the banister, both arms wrapped around a stanchion. "This whole spire may be about to break free-"»
- L1787 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Then we'll all be adrift together." Anakin glanced up at the Supreme Chancellor and for that instant he didn't like the man at all-but then he reminded himself that brave as Palpatine was, his was the courage of conviction; the man was no soldier.»
- L1789 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"His fate," he said in case Palpatine had not understood, "will be the same as ours."»
- L1791 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «With Obi-Wan unconscious and Palpatine waiting above, with responsibility for the lives of his two closest friends squarely upon him, Anakin found that he had recovered his inner balance.»
- L1795 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «The Force brought Obi-Wan's lightsaber to his hand and he clipped it to his friend's belt, then hoisted the limp body over his shoulder and let the Force help him run lightly up the steeply canted floor to Palpatine's side.»
- L1797 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Impressive," Palpatine said, but then he cast a significant gaze up the staircase, which the vector of the artificial gravity had made into a vertical cliff. "But what now?"»

**Ch5 Grievous**

- L1875 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Beside his other shoulder, Palpatine coughed harshly into the sleeve of his robe, held aver his face for protection from caustic combustion products of the overloading circuitry.»

- L1901 [SAID ABOUT (by Anakin Skywalker / Darth Vader)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Once Palpatine was safely within the shaft-hall, Anakin said, "Follow me," and turned to go, but the Chancellor stopped him with a hand on his arm.»

- L1915 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Think of it, Anakin." Palpatine stood close by his shoulder, opposite to Obi-Wan, so close he needed only to whisper.»
- L1917 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «He turned a sharp glance toward Palpatine.»

- L1929 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"I will never be safe while Grievous lives," Palpatine countered. "Master Kenobi will recover at any moment. Leave him here with me; he can see me safely to the hangar deck. Go for the general."»

**Ch6 Rescue**

- L2021 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Anakin counted paces as he trotted along the turbolift shaft, Obi-Wan over his shoulder and Palpatine at his side.»
- L2025 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «He put out his free arm to stop the Chancellor. "This is a problem. Find something to hang on to while I get us out of here."»

- L2063 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Looking up, he discovered Anakin's legs, and his boots-and a somewhat astonishing close-up view of the Supreme Chancellor, as Palpatine seemingly balanced overhead, supported only by a white-knuckled death-grip on Anakin's ankle.»

- L2117 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «They gradually managed to untangle themselves. "Are ... all of your rescues so . . ." Palpatine gasped breathlessly. "... entertaining?"»
- L2125 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «This place looked as if it had taken a direct hit; wind howled against his back through the open hatchway where Obi-Wan stood with Chancellor Palpatine, and scraps of debris whirled into the air, blown toward space through gaps in the scorched and buckled blast doors.»
- L2127 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"None of those ships will get us anywhere!" Palpatine shouted above the wind, and Anakin had to agree. "What are we going to do?"»

- L2137 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «And let you have all the fun ? Anakin pulled the Chancellor into the hangar bay and pressed him against the wall beside the hatch. "Stay under cover until we handle the droids!"»

- L2141 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «On the far side of the bay, masses of wreckage were shifting, sliding toward the wall against which Anakin and Palpatine stood.»
- L2147 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «He unspooled a length of his utility belt's safety cable and passed the end to Palpatine. The wind made it sing. "Cinch this around your waist. Things are about to get a little wild!"»

- L2239 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Mere seconds after he sealed himself and the Chancellor within the module's heavily armored hull they would be taken aboard the fleeing ship, which would then make a series of randomized microjumps to prevent being tracked before entering the final jump to the secret base on Utapau.»

- L2257 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"It's, it's, it's the Chancellor's beacon, sir."»
- L2277 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «You will find at least one Jedi, possibly two, in the company of Chancellor Palpatine, imprisoned in a ray shield.»
- L2281 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «My orders are clear that the Chancellor is not to be harmed.»
- L2287 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «He reached over and caught Palpatine by the arm.»
- L2305 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"It's the dark side-the shadow on the Force.»
- L2309 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Dooku's death should have lifted the shadow-"»

- L2369 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «When the Jedi Master turned away to offer his lightsaber to the bodyguard droid, Anakin leaned close to the Supreme Chancellor and murmured, "So you get your way, after all."»
- L2371 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Palpatine answered with a slight, unreadable smile. "I frequently do."»
- L2405 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"This weapon is your life, Obi-Wan!" He did a credible-enough Kenobi impression that Palpatine had to smother a snort.»

- L2555 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «All eight of them suddenly seemed, inexplicably, to leap into the air, followed by Skywalker, and Palpatine, and chairs and pieces of MagnaGuards and everything else on the bridge that was not bolted to the deck, except for Kenobi, who managed to grab a control console and now was»

**Ch8 Fault Lines**

- L2727 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Emergency crews scrambled with an escape slide, and a moment later the Supreme Chancellor, Obi-Wan Kenobi, and Anakin Skywalker were all on the deck beside the burning ship, closely followed by a somewhat battered R2 unit that lifted itself down on customized maneuvering rockets.»

- L2741 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"No, no, no need," Palpatine said, rather faintly. "Thank you, Master Windu, but I am well. Quite well, thanks to these two."»

- L2759 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Mace remained expressionless as he subtly moved his arm away from the Chancellor's hand.»
- L2767 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Dooku was to the Separatists what Palpatine was to the Republic: the center of gravity binding together a spiral galaxy of special interests.»
- L2779 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Inexplicably, young Skywalker looked distinctly uncomfortable; that newly confident presence of his collapsed as suddenly as an overloaded deflector, and instead of meeting Mace's eyes, his gaze flicked to Palpatine.»
- L2779 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «He looked to the Chancellor as well, his elation sinking, becoming puzzlement tinged with suspicion.»

Especially after having disposed of Master Kenobi so neatly."»
- L2785 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Perhaps young Anakin was simply more . . . highly motivated," Palpatine said, turning a fond smile upon him. - L2789 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «No one on the Jedi Council had ever been comfortable with Skywalker's close relationship with the Chancellor-they'd had more than one conversation about it with Obi-Wan while Skywalker had still been his Padawan-and Mace was less than happy to hear Palpatine speaking for a young Jedi who seemed unprepared to speak for himself.»
- L2795 [SAID ABOUT (by Anakin Skywalker / Darth Vader)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Without Dooku to hold the coalition together, these so-called independent systems will splinter, and they know it." He looked straight into the Supreme Chancellor's eyes.»
- L2797 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «And while Palpatine answered, Mace Windu reached into the Force.»
- L2801 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «On the day of Palpatine's election to the Chancellorship, he had seen that Palpatine was himself a shatterpoint of unimaginable significance: a man upon whom might depend the fate of the Republic itself.»
- L2805 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Anakin was somehow a pivot point, the fulcrum of a lever with Obi-Wan on one side, Palpatine on the other, and the galaxy in the balance, but the dark cloud on the Force prevented his perception from reaching into the future for so much as a hint of where this might lead.»

I'm afraid that, far from being over, this war is about to get a very great deal worse."»

- L2825 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Absolutely not." Palpatine drew himself up, straightening his robes. "A negotiated peace would be a recognition of the»
- L2829 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Then the Jedi will make the capture of General Grievous our particular task." He glanced at Anakin and Obi-Wan, then back to Palpatine.»
- L2829 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «He leaned close to the Chancellor and his voice went low and final, with a buried intensity that hinted-just the slightest bit-of suspicion, and warning.»

- L2833 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «As the Jedi shuttle swung toward the Chancellor's private landing platform at the Senate Offices, Obi-Wan watched Anakin pretending not to stare out the window.»
- L2837 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «The Korun Master made a nearly invisible gesture, to which Obi-Wan did not visibly respond; but when Palpatine and Anakin and R2 all debarked toward the crowd of well-wishers, Obi-Wan stayed behind.»
- L2851 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «You killed Count Dooku, and single-handedly rescued the Chancellor . . . all while, I might be forgiven for adding, carrying some old broken-down Jedi Master unconscious on your back.»
- L2861 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Just this once." Anakin laughed and waved, and then headed briskly off to catch up with Palpatine as the Chancellor waded into the Senators with the smooth-as-oiled-transparisteel ease of the lifelong politician.»
- L2865 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «I don't like his relationship with Palpatine."»
- L2873 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «I only wish we could trust the Chancellor with Anakin's."»
- L2875 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Palpatine's policies are ... sometimes questionable.»
- L2887 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Be mindful of Anakin, and be careful of Palpatine.»
- L2893 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «We have circumstantial evidence that traces Sidious to Palpatine's inner circle."»
- L2897 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «But this raid-the capture of Palpatine had to be an inside job.»
- L2897 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Its inhabitants included only the incredibly wealthy or the incredibly powerful, from Raith Sienar of the Sienar Systems conglomerate to Palpatine himself.»
- L2899 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «That the Senate under the influence-under the control-of Darth Sidious.»
- L2905 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «All we know of Sidious is that he's bipedal, of roughly human conformation.»
- L2909 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Interrogate the Supreme Chancellor's personal aides and advisors?»
- L2913 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Palpatine will never allow it.»
- L2921 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Relations with the Chancellor's Office are ... difficult.»
- L2929 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"The only reason Palpatine's not a suspect is because he already rules the galaxy."»
- L2931 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «I would think that Anakin's friendship with Palpatine could be of use to us in this-he has the kind of access to Palpatine that other Jedi might only dream of.»
- L2941 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «The less he has to do with Palpatine, the better."»
- L2943 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «He thought of the danger Palpatine faced unknowingly, with a Sith Lord among his advisers . . .»
- L2953 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"It is possible that we may have to ... move against Palpatine."»
- L2961 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «You don't know what it's been like, dealing with all the petty squabbles and special interests and greedy, grasping fools in the Senate, and Palpatine's constant, cynical, ruthless maneuvering for power-he carves away chunks of our freedom and bandages the wounds with tiny scraps of security.»
- L3039 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «He felt a presence at his shoulder and turned to find Palpatine beside him with a warm smile and a soft word, as he always seemed to be when Anakin was troubled.»
- L3041 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"What is it, Anakin?" the Chancellor asked kindly. "Something is disturbing you. I can tell."»
- L3045 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Anakin, anything that might upset a man such as yourself is certainly something. Let me help."»
- L3049 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Ah. The Jedi Council." Palpatine slid an arm around Anakin's shoulders and gave him a comradely squeeze. "I believe I can be of some use to you in this problem after all."»
- L3053 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"I should be very much surprised if I couldn't."»
- L3055 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Palpatine's smile was still warm, but his eyes had gone distant.»

**Ch9 Padme**

- L3063 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «From the shadow of a great pillar stretching up into the red-ening afternoon that leaked through the vaulted roof of transparisteel over the Atrium of the Senate Office Building, she watched»
- L3067 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Through the swirl of HoloNet reporters and the chatter of Senators and the gently comforting tones of Palpatine's most polished, reassuringly paternal voice, she did not move, not so much as to lift a hand or turn her head.»
- L3071 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «pacing in soft conversation with Bail Organa of Alderaan, and she heard Bail saying something about the end of Count Dooku and the end of the war and finally an end to Palpatine's police-state tactics, her breath caught again and she held it, because she knew the next thing she heard would be his voice.»

**Ch10 Masters**

- L3337 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"By placing the Jedi Council under the control of the Office of the Supreme Chancellor, this amendment will give him the constitutional authority to disband the Order itself."»
- L3357 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"We don't know what the Sith Lord's plans may be," Obi-Wan said, "but we can be certain that Palpatine is not to be trusted.»
- L3357 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «This draft resolution is not the product of some overzealous Senator; we may be sure Palpatine wrote it himself and passed it along to someone he controls-to make it-look like the Senate is once more 'forcing him to reluctantly accept extra powers in the name of security.'»
- L3359 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «The dark side of the Force surrounds the Chancellor."»
- L3361 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «If the Chancellor is being influenced through the dark side, this whole war may have been, from the beginning, a plot by the Sith to destroy the Jedi Order."»
- L3371 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Palpatine may be about to do what the Separatists cannot: bring down the Republic.»
- L3387 [SAID ABOUT (by Yoda)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Working behind the scenes we should be, to uncover Lord Sidious!»
- L3387 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «To move against Palpatine while the Sith still exist-this may be part of the Sith plan itself, to turn the Senate and the public against the Jedi!»
- L3399 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"It seems to me," he said carefully, "that Palpatine himself has given us an opening.»
- L3399 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «It will draw Sidious out; he will have to make some sort of move, if he wishes the war to continue."»
- L3401 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"The war has been a Sith operation from the beginning, with Dooku on one side and Sidious on the other-it has always been a plot aimed at us.»
- L3413 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Yoda and I will remain on Coruscant, monitoring Palpatine's advisers and lackeys; we'll move against Sidi-ous the instant he is revealed.»
- L3437 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «He had no time for lengthy explanations; he was expecting a secret transmission from Sidious himself.»
- L3437 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Grievous still hoped, every day, that Lord Sidious would give him leave to smash the skulls of Gunray and his toady, Rune Haako.»
- L3471 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"You tell us we'll be safe on Mustafar-but you also told us you would deliver Palpatine as a hostage, and he managed to escape your grip!"»
- L3483 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Yes, Lord Sidious."»
- L3503 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «If I may trouble you with boldness-why did you not let me kill Chancellor Palpatine?»
- L3519 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Far across the expanse of deck stood the Supreme Chancellor's shuttle.»
- L3523 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «He'd scoured the Temple for his former Master; he'd nearly giving up hope of finding him when a passing Padawan had mentioned that he'd seen Obi-Wan on his way out to the landing deck to meet Palpatine's shuttle.»
- L3573 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «The shadow of greed, attachment is.»
- L3605 [SAID ABOUT (by Anakin Skywalker / Darth Vader)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Is Palpatine here?" Anakin asked.»
- L3607 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"That shuttle did not bring the Chancellor.»
- L3615 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «When the deck-duty Padawans questioned them, they said the Chancellor has requested your presence."»
- L3619 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Relations between the Council and the Chancellor are . . . stressed."»
- L3635 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «I am your friend, and as your friend, I am asking you: be wary of Palpatine."»
- L3655 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «The ride to Palpatine's office was quietly tense.»
- L3657 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Anakin's discomfort only increased when he arrived at Palpatine's office.»
- L3657 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «that he didn't even really see it, most times: the deep red runner that matched the softly curving walls, the long comfortable couches, the huge arc of window behind Palpatine's desk-these were all so familiar that they were usually almost invisible, but today-Today, with Obi-Wan's voice whispering be wary of Palpatine in the back of his head,»
- L3659 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «The light of the Chancellor's lampdisks seemed brighter than usual, almost harsh, but somehow that only deepened the gloom.»
- L3659 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «He discovered now an odd, accidental echo of memory, a new harmonic resonance inside his head, when he looked at the curving view wall that threw into silhouette the Chancellor's single large chair.»
- L3661 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Palpatine's office reminded him of the General's Quarters on Invisible Hand.»
- L3663 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «And it struck him as unaccountably sinister that the robes worn by the Chancellor's cadre of bodyguards were the exact color of Palpatine's carpet.»
- L3665 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Palpatine himself stood at the view wall, hands clasped behind him, gazing out upon the smoke-hazed morning.»
- L3671 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Do you see, Anakin?" Palpatine's voice was soft, hoarse with emotion. "Do you see what they have done to our magnificent city? This war must end. We cannot allow such . . . such ..."»
- L3673 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Gently, Anakin laid a hand on Palpatine's shoulder, and a hint of frown fleeted over his face at how frail seemed the flesh and bone beneath the robe.»
- L3675 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Palpatine nodded, lowering his head. "I know I have yours, Anakin. The rest of the Jedi . . ." He sighed. He looked even more exhausted than he had yesterday. Perhaps he had passed a sleepless night as well.»
- L3679 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Anakin went still for a moment, then he very slowly lifted his hand from the Chancellor's shoulder.»
- L3681 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Be wary of Palpatine»
- L3691 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «And, I suppose, wiser." He didn't want to get into this with Palpatine; talking with the Chancellor like this-seriously, man-to-man-made him feel good, feel strong, despite Obi-Wan's warning.»
- L3701 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"I have asked you here today, Anakin, because I have fears of my own." He turned, waiting, until Anakin met his eye, and on Palpatine's face was something approaching bleak despair.»
- L3705 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «But Palpatine had already turned away.»
- L3709 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Be wary of Palpatine, he'd said, and be careful of your feelings. . .»
- L3715 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Oh, I have no doubt of it," Palpatine was saying. "Many of the Jedi on your Council would prefer I was out of office altogether-because they know I'm on to them, now. They're shrouded in secrecy, obsessed with covert action against mysteriously faceless enemies-"»
- L3721 [SAID ABOUT (by Anakin Skywalker / Darth Vader)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"But Sidious . . ."»
- L3723 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Ah, yes, the mysterious Lord Sidious.»
- L3723 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «How do you know this Sidious even exists?»
- L3735 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Listen to me: if this 'Darth Sidious' of yours were to walk through that door right now-and I could somehow stop you from killing him on the spot-do you know what I would do?"»
- L3737 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Palpatine rose, and his voice rose with him. "I would ask him to sit down, and I would ask him if he has any power he could use to end this war!"»
- L3741 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"And if he said he did, I'd bloody well offer him a brandy and talk it out!\"»
- L3745 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Well, not entirely." Palpatine sighed, and shrugged, and lowered himself once more into his chair. "It's only an example, Anakin. I would do anything to return peace to the galaxy, do you understand? That's all I mean. After all-" He offered a tired, sadly ironic smile. "-what are the chances of an actual Sith Lord ever walking through that door?"»
- L3749 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Oh, yes." Palpatine chuckled. "Yes, quite right. They might take it as an excuse to accuse me."»
- L3753 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"I am not. I am no longer sure they'll stop at anything, Anakin. That's actually the reason I asked you here today." He leaned forward intently, resting his elbows on the desk. "You may have heard that this afternoon, the Senate will call upon this office to assume direct control of the Jedi Council."»
- L3757 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"They will report to me. Personally. The Senate is too unfocused to conduct this war; we've seen this for years. Now that this office will be the single authority to direct the prosecution of the war, we'll bring a quick end to things."»
- L3769 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Inside his head, he heard the echo of Obi-Wan, murmuring relations between the Council and the Chancellor are . . . stressed.»
- L3775 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «One of Palpatine's eyebrows arched. "Their actions will speak more loudly than their words-as long as someone keeps an eye on them. And that, my boy, is exactly the favor I must ask of you."»
- L3781 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Who else?" Palpatine spread his hands in a melancholy shrug. "You are the only Jedi I know, truly know, that I can trust.»
- L3791 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Palpatine had somehow seen into his secret heart, and had chosen to offer him the one thing he most desired in all the galaxy.»
- L3803 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"I promise you they will," Palpatine murmured imperturbably. He swung his chair around to gaze out the window toward the distant spires of the Temple. "They need you more than they realize. All it will take is for someone to properly ..."»

**Ch11 Politics**

- L3819 [SAID ABOUT (by Yoda)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"An unintentional opportunity, the Chancellor has given us," Yoda said gravely.»
- L3825 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Don't make him choose between me and Palpatine-"»
- L3829 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"You don't know how much Palpatine's friendship has meant to him over the years.»
- L3829 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Don't you understand what this will cost him, even if Palpatine is entirely innocent?»
- L3831 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «I have told you all what I have seen of the energy between Skywalker and the Supreme Chancellor.»
- L3831 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Anything that might distance young Skywalker from Palpatine's influence is worth the attempt."»
- L3839 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Anakin will certainly report this to the Chancellor's Office.»
- L3839 [SAID ABOUT (by Yoda)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Perhaps, as you say, that will draw Sidious into action."»
- L3841 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Let us take this one step farther-we should appear shorthanded, and weak, giving Sidious an opening to make a move he thinks will go unobserved.»
- L3841 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «I'm thinking that perhaps we should let the Chancellor's Office know that Yoda and I have both been forced to take the field-"»
- L3847 [SAID ABOUT (by Yoda)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «And good relations with the Wookiees I have; destroy the droid armies I can, and still be available to Coruscant, should Sidious take our bait."»
- L3849 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"And one last touch Let's let the Chancellor know, through Anakin, that our most cunning and insightful Master-and our most tenacious-is to lead the hunt for Grievous."»
- L3851 [SAID ABOUT (by Yoda)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"So Sidious will need to act, and act fast, if the war is to be maintained," Plo Koon added approvingly.»
- L3861 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «He stared up in disbelief at one of the huge holoprojected Proclamation Boards; these had recently been installed above the concourse to keep the thousands of Senators up to the moment on news of the war, and on the Chancellor's latest executive orders.»
- L3865 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Since yesterday, when the Senate had voted to give Palpatine control of the Jedi, he'd known it would come soon.»
- L3891 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"She is also a longtime associate of Palpatine," he reminded her.»
- L3921 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"The Council has decided to comply with Chancellor Palpatine's directive, and with the instructions of the Senate that give him the unprecedented authority to command this Council.»
- L3921 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «You are hereby granted a seat at the High Council of the Jedi, as the Chancellor's personal representative."»
- L3925 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Palpatine had been right.»
- L3925 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «In fact-now that Anakin came to think of it-he couldn't remember a single instance when the Supreme Chancellor had been wrong.»
- L3931 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Disturbing is this move by Chancellor Palpatine.»
- L3959 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"This is an insult to me, and to the Chancellor.»
- L3995 [SAID ABOUT (by Padme Amidala)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"But I've known Palpatine for years; he was my most trusted adviser.»
- L4001 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Palpatine no longer has to worry about controlling the Senate.»
- L4015 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"It has become increasingly clear," Mon Mothma said, "that Palpatine has become an enemy of democracy.»
- L4019 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Who will vote against Palpatine now?"»
- L4025 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Make as much noise as you can-keep Palpatine watching what you're doing in the Senate.»
- L4031 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Until then, this meeting must remain absolutely secret Even hinting at an effective opposition to Palpatine can be as we've all seen, very dangerous.»
- L4035 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «You can keep your own secrets, but I'll have to tell my Jedi husband, who is Palpatine's beloved protege . . .»
- L4047 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"It's your friendship with the Chancellor-the same friendship that got you a seat at the Council-that makes it impossible to grant you Mastery.»
- L4047 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «In the Council's eyes, that would be the same as giving a vote to Palpatine himself!"»
- L4049 [SAID ABOUT (by Padme Amidala)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «So if I wasn't friends with Palpatine I'd be a Master already, is that what you're saying?"»
- L4059 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «I told you of the . . . tension . . . between the Council and the Chancellor.»
- L4073 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Who would have killed Dooku, and rescued the Chancellor?»
- L4075 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «It's your relationship to Palpatine that is the problem.»
- L4103 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"The Council," Obi-Wan said slowly, "approved your appointment because Palpatine trusts you.»
- L4105 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"They want me to spy on the Supreme Chancellor of the Republic?" Anakin blinked numbly.»
- L4107 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Especially when the greatest enemy of those principles seems to be the Chancellor himself!"»
- L4113 [SAID ABOUT (by Anakin Skywalker / Darth Vader)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"What I understand," Anakin said grimly, "is that you are trying to turn me against Palpatine.»
- L4155 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Listen: we're not asking you to act against Palpatine.»
- L4159 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Palpatine himself may be in danger," he said.»
- L4167 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Master Windu traced Darth Sidious to Five Hundred Republica before Grievous's attack-we think that the Sith Lord is someone within Palpatine's closest circle of advisers.»
- L4171 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"If Palpatine is under the influence of a Sith Lord, he may be in the gravest danger.»
- L4171 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «The only way we can help him is to find Sidious, and to stop him.»
- L4173 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «If this Darth Sidious of yours were to walk through that door right now ...»
- L4175 [SAID ABOUT (by Anakin Skywalker / Darth Vader)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"So all you're really asking," Anakin said slowly, "is for me to help the Council find Darth Sidious."»

**Ch12 Not from a Jedi**

- L4299 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «I'm on my way to meet with the Chancellor."»
- L4305 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"This is just political maneuvering between the Council and the Chancellor I got caught in the middle, that's all."»
- L4339 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Where would the Republic be without Palpatine?"»
- L4343 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Everybody complains about Palpatine having too much power, but nobody offers a better alternative.»
- L4353 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Palpatine's your friend, he might listen to you.»
- L4359 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «What if I think Palpatine's way is the right way?"»
- L4381 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «He'd seen something in her eyes, when he'd mentioned the Council and Palpatine.»
- L4399 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Two masked, robed, silent Red Guards flanked the door to the Chancellor's private box at the Galaxies Opera.»
- L4403 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «In the dim semi-gloom, Palpatine sat with the speaker of the Senate, Mas Amedda, and his administrative aide, Sly Moore.»
- L4409 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Palpatine turned toward him, and his face lit up. "Yes, Anakin! Don't worry. Come in, my boy, come in. Thank you for your report on the Council meeting this afternoon-it made most interesting reading. And now I have good news for you-Clone Intelligence has located General Grievous!"»
- L4425 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Do you still? Sit down." Palpatine looked at the other two beings in the box. "Leave us."»
- L4429 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Palpatine gazed distractedly down at the graceful undulations of the Mon Calamari principal soloist for a long moment, frowning as though there was so much he wanted to say, he was unsure where to begin.»
- L4433 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"You must sense what I have come to suspect," Palpatine said grimly. "The Jedi Council is after more than independence from Senate oversight; I believe they intend to control the Republic itself."»
- L4437 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"I believe they are planning treason. They hope to overthrow my government, and replace me with someone weak enough that Jedi mind tricks can control his every word."»
- L4447 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Have they ordered you to do something dishonest?" Palpatine's frown cleared into a gently wise smile that was oddly reminiscent of Yoda's.»
- L4453 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Do you remember," Palpatine said, drawing away from Anakin so that he could lean back comfortably in his seat, "how as a young boy, when you first came to this planet, I tried to teach you the ins and outs of politics?"»
- L4457 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"For any lessons, as I recall. But it's a pity; you should have paid more attention. To understand politics is to understand the fundamental nature of thinking beings. Right now, you should remember one of my first teachings: all those who gain power are afraid to lose it."»
- L4461 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Good is a point of view, Anakin. And the Jedi concept of good is not the only valid one. Take your Dark Lords of the Sith, for example. From my reading, I have gathered that the Sith believed in justice and security every bit as much as the Jedi-"»
- L4465 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"In these troubled times, is there a difference?" Palpatine asked mildly. "The Jedi have not done a stellar job of bringing peace to the galaxy, you must agree. Who's to say the Sith might not have done better?"»
- L4469 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Oh, yes. Because the Sith would be a threat to the Jedi Order's power. Lesson one."»
- L4473 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"From a Jedi's point of view," Palpatine allowed. "Evil is a label we all put on those who threaten us, isn't it? Yet the Sith and the Jedi are similar in almost every way, including their quest for greater power."»
- L4477 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Which brings with it greater power, does it not?"»
- L4481 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"We're not arguing, Anakin. We're just talking." Palpatine shifted his weight, settling in comfortably. "Perhaps the real difference between the Jedi and the Sith lies only in their orientation; a Jedi gains power through understanding, and a Sith gains understanding through power. This is the true reason the Sith have always been more powerful»
- L4497 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Palpatine again gave him that smile of gentle wisdom. "Or so you've been trained to believe. I hear the voice of Obi-Wan Kenobi in your answers, Anakin. What do you really think?"»
- L4499 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Anakin suddenly found the ballet a great deal more interesting than Palpatine's face.»
- L4505 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"What the Jedi are," Palpatine said gently, "is a group of very powerful beings you consider to be your comrades. And you are loyal to your friends; I have known that for as long as I have known you, and I admire you for it. But are your friends loyal to you?"»
- L4509 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Would a true friend ask you to do something that's wrong?"»
- L4511 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «They might only want to catch Sidious.»
- L4511 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «They might really be trying to protect Palpatine.»
- L4525 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Except for what Palpatine said.»
- L4529 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"This puts me in mind of an old legend," Palpatine murmured idly. "Anakin-are you familiar with The Tragedy of Darth Plagueis the Wise?"»
- L4539 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"According to the legend," Palpatine said, "he could directly influence the midi-chlorians to create life; with such knowledge, to maintain life in someone already living would seem a small matter, don't you agree?"»
- L4543 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"The dark side seems to be-from my reading-the pathway to many abilities some would consider unnatural."»
- L4547 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Oh, well, it is a tragedy, after all, you know. Once he has gained this ultimate power, he has nothing to fear save losing it-that's why the Jedi Council brought him to mind, you know."»
- L4551 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Well, to safeguard his power's existence, he teaches the path toward it to his apprentice."»
- L4555 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"And his apprentice kills him in his sleep," Palpatine said with a careless shrug. "Plageuis never sees it coming. That's the tragic irony, you see: he can save anyone in the galaxy from death-except himself."»
- L4559 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Oh, him. He goes on to become the greatest Dark Lord the Sith have ever known . . ."»
- L4563 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Oh, well, yes. Quite right. I'd never really thought of it that way-rather like what we were talking about earlier, isn't it?"»
- L4567 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"I'm sorry?"»
- L4571 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Oh, I am ... rather certain . . . that Plagueis did indeed exist. And if someone actually had this power-well, he would indeed be one of the most powerful men in the galaxy, not to mention virtually immortal ..."»
- L4575 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"I'm sure I couldn't say. You could ask your friends on the Jedi Council, I suppose-but of course, if they ever found him they'd kill him on the spot. Not as punishment for any crime, you understand. Innocence is irrelevant to the Jedi. They would kill him simply for being Sith, and his knowledge would die with him."»
- L4579 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Palpatine shrugged, regarding him with that smile of gentle wisdom.»

**Ch13 The Will of the Force**

- L4645 [SAID ABOUT] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"He's been put in a difficult position as the Chancellor's representative, but I think there's more to it than that.»
- L4737 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Anakin had brought the holoprojector from the Chancellor's office; Obi-Wan wondered idly if the projector had been scanned for recording devices planted by the Chancellor to spy on their meeting, then dismissed the thought.»
- L4737 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «In a sense, Anakin was the Chancellor's recording device.»
- L4745 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"How could the Chancellor have come by this information when we know nothing about it?"»
- L4751 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan felt a frown crawl onto his forehead at the way Anakin now referred to the Chancellor's Office as we . . .»
- L4753 [NARRATION] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «The constitutional amendment bringing the Jedi under the Chancellor's Office naturally includes troops commanded by Jedi.»
- L4753 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «Palpatine is now Supreme Commander of the Grand Army of the Republic."»
- L4759 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Sidious, concealed as Palpatine, 19 BBY] «"Not the Chancellor."»

- L1115 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku sighed. He should, he supposed, have no difficulty with this, having ordered the Jedi Master's death once already. "True enough, I suppose; how fortunate we are that I never labored under any such illusions."»
- L1119 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku understood: not only would the death of his mentor tip Skywalker's already unstable emotional balance down the darkest of slopes, but it would also remove the greatest obstacle to Skywalker's successful conversion.»
- L1121 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Still, though, Dooku had some reservations.»
- L1125 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «"Which is precisely," Dooku said meditatively, "why it might be best if I were to kill him, instead."»
- L1129 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «"Please. Of what use is power unstructured by discipline? The boy is as much a danger to himself as he is to his enemies. And that mechanical arm-" Dooku's lip curled with cultivated distaste. "Revolting."»
- L1133 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «"Hmp. A gentleman would have learned to fight one-handed." Dooku flicked a dismissive wave. "He's no longer even entirely human. With Grievous, the use of these bio-droid devices is almost forgivable; he was such a disgusting creature already that his mechanical parts are clearly an improvement. But a blend of droid and human? Appalling. The»
- L1137 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku lifted an eyebrow. "I have overstepped, my Master," he said with his customary grace. "I am only observing, not arguing. Not at all."»
- L1141 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku could not argue.»
- L1141 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Not only had the Dark Lord introduced Dooku to realms of power beyond his most spectacular fantasies, but Sidious was also a political manipulator so subtle that his abilities might be considered to dwarf even the power of the dark side itself.»
- L1149 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku understood that this was more than a test for Skywalker; though Sidious had never said so directly, Dooku was certain that he himself was being tested as well.»
- L1157 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku nodded.»
- L1157 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «For the first time since Sidious had revealed the true subtlety of this masterpiece, Dooku allowed himself to relax enough to imagine the outcome.»
- L1159 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «With his heroic capture of Count Dooku, Anakin Skywalker will become the ultimate hero: the greatest hero in the history of he Republic, perhaps of the Jedi Order itself.»
- L1165 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku could only shake his head in awe.»
- L1177 [SAID ABOUT] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «"Tyranus?»
- L1179 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «"Am I . . ." Dooku realized that his eyes had misted. "Yes, my Master. I am beyond well. Today, the climax-the grand finale the culmination of all your decades of work ... I find myself somewhat overcome."»
- L1181 [SAID ABOUT] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «"Compose yourself, Tyranus.»
- L1183 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku straightened and for the first time looked his Master in the eyes.»
- L1187 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku bowed to him. "Thank you, Chancellor."»

**Ch3 The Way Of The Sith**

- L1205 [SAID ABOUT (by Anakin Skywalker / Darth Vader)] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «"And Dooku?"»
- L1217 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Just remember that Dooku is no mere Dark Jedi like that Ventress woman; he is a Lord of the Sith.»
- L1255 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Inside a turbolift pod, Dooku watched hologrammic images of Kenobi and Skywalker cautiously pick their way down the curving stairs from the entrance balcony to the main level of the General's Quarters, moving slowly to stay braced against the pitching of the cruiser.»
- L1259 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku nodded judiciously to himself, frowning down at the translucent blue ghosts slinking toward Palpatine.»
- L1263 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «"Indeed you are, you vile creature," Dooku muttered to the dead comlink. "Out of luck, and out of time."»
- L1269 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku straightened his cloak of shimmering armorweave and strode grandly into the half-dark lift lobby.»
- L1269 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku sighed and gestured, and the opalescent wreckage of the door silently slid itself out of his way.»
- L1291 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «All he could imagine was what Grievous and Dooku must have done to put fear on the face of this brave good man-And that imagining ignited a sizzle in his blood that drew his face tight and clouded his heart and started again the low roll of thunder in his ears: thunder from Aargonar.»
- L1313 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Count Dooku's voice.»
- L1319 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «The entrance balcony provided an appropriate angle-far above the Jedi, looking down upon them-for Dooku to make final assessments before beginning the farce.»
- L1321 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Like all true farce, the coming denouement would proceed with remorseless logic from its ridiculous premise: that Dooku could ever be overcome by mere Jedi.»
- L1323 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku had always preferred an educated audience.»
- L1329 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Skywalker gave Dooku only his back, but his blade was already out and his tall, lean frame stood frozen with anticipation: so motionless he almost seemed to shiver.»
- L1331 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «He simply stood gazing calmly up at Dooku and the super battle droids that flanked him, hands open, utterly relaxed, on his face only an expression of mild interest.»
- L1333 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku derived a certain melancholy satisfaction-a pleasurably lonely contemplation of his own unrecognized greatness-from a brief reflection that Skywalker would never understand how much thought and planning, how much work, Lord Sidious had invested in so hastily orchestrating his sham victory.»
- L1333 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Nor would he ever understand the artistry, the true mastery, that Dooku would wield in his own defeat.»
- L1345 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Powered by the dark side, Dooku's perception took the measure of those below him with exhilarating precision.»
- L1357 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «"Get help!" The edge of panic in his hoarse half whisper sounded real even to Dooku. "You must get help. Neither of you is any match for a Sith Lord!"»
- L1359 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Now Skywalker turned, meeting Dooku's direct gaze for the first time since the abandoned hangar on Geonosis.»
- L1359 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «His reply was clearly intended as much for Dooku as for Palpatine.»
- L1363 [SAID ABOUT] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «"Anakin-" In the Force, Dooku could feel Kenobi's disapproval of Skywalker's boasting; and he could also feel Kenobi's effortless self-restraint in focusing on the matter at hand.»
- L1365 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku's sharp eye picked up the tightening of Skywalker's droid hand on his lightsaber's grip.»
- L1369 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku leaned forward, and his cloak of armorweave spread like wings; he lifted gently into the air and descended to the main level in a slow, dignified Force-glide.»
- L1373 [SAID ABOUT] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «"You won't escape us this time, Dooku."»
- L1375 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «"Escape you? Please." Dooku allowed his customary mild smile to spread. "Do you think I orchestrated this entire operation with the intent to escape? I could have taken the Chancellor outsystem hours ago. But I have better things to do with my life than to babysit him while I wait for the pair of you to attempt a rescue."»
- L1379 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «"And a little less than a rescue."»

The lad's personal issues were too tiresome to pursue; he knew far too much already about Skywalker's messy private life. "I bear Chancellor Palpatine no ill will, foolish boy. He is neither soldier nor spy, whereas you and your friend here are both. It is only an unfortunate accident of history that he has chosen to defend»
- L1395 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «"The Chancellor is a civilian. You and General Kenobi, on the other hand, are legitimate military targets. "-or as corpses.''»
- L1397 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «"Now, there's a coincidence," Kenobi replied dryly as he swung around Dooku to place the Count precisely between Skywalker and himself.»

- L1407 [SAID ABOUT] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «"Is he?" Dooku relaxed.»

- L1419 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «The ship shuddered and the red smoke surged from Anakin's spine into his arms and legs and head and when Dooku gave the slightest glance of concern over his shoulder, distracted for half an instant, Anakin just couldn't wait anymore.»
- L1423 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan leapt from Dooku's far side in perfect coordination-and they met in midair, for the Sith Lord was no longer between them.»

- L1431 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «And Dooku barely even seemed to be paying attention to him.»

- L1443 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Ah, Dooku thought.»

- L1451 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «"Really," Dooku said, "this is pathetic."»
- L1453 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Oh, they were certainly energetic enough, leaping and whirling, raining blows almost at random, cutting chairs to pieces and Force-hurling them in every conceivable direction, while Dooku continued, in his gracefully methodical way, to out-maneuver them so thoroughly it was all he could to do keep from laughing out loud.»

- L1457 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «They were not prepared to fight together against a single Force-user, certainly not one of Dooku's power; he, on the other hand, had always fought alone.»

- L1477 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku thought, What?»

- L1479 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Skywalker slammed his following strike down so hard that the shock of deflecting it buckled Dooku's elbows.»
- L1479 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku threw himself into a backroll that brought him to his feet-and Kenobi's blade was there to meet his neck.»
