- L1481 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «The second bent Dooku's wrist.»
- L1481 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «The third flash of blue forced Dooku's scarlet blade so far to the inside that his own lightsaber scorched his shoulder, and Dooku was forced to give ground.»
- L1483 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku felt himself blanch.»
- L1483 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku backed away as fast as he dared; Skywalker stayed right on top of him.»
- L1483 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku's breath went short and hard.»
- L1483 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «He no longer tried to block Skywalker's strikes but only to guide them slanting away; he could not meet Skywalker strength-to-strength-not only did the boy wield tremendous reserves of Force energy, but his sheer physical power was astonishing-And only then did Dooku understand that he'd been suckered.»
- L1485 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Skywalker's Shien ready-stance had been a ruse, as had his Ataro gymnastics; the boy was a Djem So stylist, and as fine a one as Dooku had ever seen.»
- L1489 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «He dropped low and spun into another reverse ankle-sweep-the weakness of Djem So was its lack of mobility-that slapped Skywalker's boot sharply enough to throw the young Jedi off balance, giving Dooku the opportunity to leap away-Only to find himself again facing the wheel of blue lightning that was Kenobi's blade.»
- L1491 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku decided that the comedy had ended.»
- L1495 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Kenobi's Master had been Qui-Gon Jinn, Dooku's own Padawan; Dooku had fenced Qui-Gon thousands of times, and he knew every weakness of the Ataro form, with its ridiculous acrobatics.»
- L1495 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «He drove a series of flashing thrusts toward Kenobi's legs to draw the Jedi Master into a flipping overhead leap so that Dooku could burn through his spine from kidneys to shoulder blades-and this image, this plan, was so clear in Dooku's mind that he almost failed to notice that Kenobi met every one of his»
- L1499 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku found himself having a sudden, unexpected, overpowering, and entirely distressing bad feeling about this . . .»
- L1501 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Realization burst through Dooku's consciousness like the blossoming fireballs of dying ships outside: this pair of Jedi fools had somehow managed to become entirely dangerous.»
- L1507 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «He gathered the Force once more in a single indrawn breath that summoned power from throughout the universe; the slightest whipcrack of that power, negligent as a flick of his wrist, sent Kenobi flying backward to crash hard against the wall, but Dooku didn't have time to enjoy it.»
- L1511 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «The shining blue lightsaber whirled and spat and every overhand chop crashed against Dooku's defense with the unstoppable power of a meteor strike; the Sith Lord spent lavishly of his reserve of the Force merely to meet these attacks without being cut in half, and Skywalker-Skywalker was getting stronger.»
- L1513 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Each parry cost Dooku more power than he'd used to throw Kenobi across the room; each block aged him a decade.»
- L1519 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «That blue blade was everywhere, flashing and whirling faster and faster until Dooku saw the room through an electric haze and now Kenobi was back in the picture: with a shout of the Force, he shot like a torpedo up the stairs behind Skywalker, and Dooku decided that»
- L1525 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Before their pieces could even hit the floor Dooku was in motion, landing a spinning side-stamp that folded Skywalker in half; he used his last burst of dark power to continue his spin into a blindingly fast wheel-kick that brought»
- L1531 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «While Kenobi's bonelessly limp body was still tumbling toward the floor far below, Dooku sent a surge of energy through the Force.»
- L1533 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «This Dooku found exceedingly gratifying.»
- L1535 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Now, as for Skywalker-Which was as far as Dooku got, because by the time his attention returned to the younger Jedi, his vision was rather completely obstructed by the sole of a boot approaching his face with something resembling terminal velocity.»
- L1541 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «He dusted himself off and fixed a supercilious gaze on Skywalker, who now stood upon the balcony looking down at him-and Dooku couldn't hold the stare; he found this reversal of their original positions oddly unsettling.»
- L1543 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Seeing Skywalker standing where Dooku himself had stood only moments ago ... it was as though he was trying to remember a dream he'd never actually had . . .»
- L1549 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Even as the boy hurtled downward, Dooku felt a new twist in the currents of the Force between them, and he finally understood.»
- L1559 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «And even now, he was holding himself back; even now, as he landed at Dooku's flank and rained blows upon the Sith Lord's defenses, even as he drove Dooku backward step after step, Dooku could feel how Skywalker kept his fury banked behind walls of will: walls that were hardened by some uncontrollable dread.»
- L1561 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dread, Dooku surmised, of himself.»
- L1563 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku slipped aside from an overhand chop and sprang backward. "I sense great fear in you. You are consumed by it. Hero With No Fear, indeed. You're a fraud, Skywalker. You are nothing but a posturing child."»

- L1569 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku allowed himself to relax; he felt that spirit of playfulness coming over him again as he and Skywalker spun 'round each other in their lethal dance.»
- L1575 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku thought blankly, Kill me?»
- L1577 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «He and Skywalker paused for one single, final instant, blades locked together, staring at each other past a sizzling cross of scarlet against blue, and in that instant Dooku found himself wondering in bewildered astonishment if Sidious had suddenly lost his mind.»

**Ch4 Jedi Trap**

- L1585 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «This is the death of Count Dooku:»
- L1593 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku is dead already.»
- L1595 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku & Skywalker, a one-time-only command performance, for an audience of one.»
- L1601 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku's decades of combat experience are irrelevant.»
- L1605 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku's elegant farce has degenerated into bathetic melodrama, and not one shed tear will mark the passing of its hero.»
- L1609 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «That imaginary dead-star dragon tries its best to freeze away his strength, to whisper him that Dooku has beaten him before, that Dooku has all the power of the darkness, to remind him how Dooku took his hand, how Dooku could strike down even Obi-Wan himself seemingly without effort»
- L1611 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «When Count Dooku flies at him, blade flashing, Watto's fist cracks out from Anakin's childhood to knock the Sith Lord tumbling back.»
- L1613 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «When with all the power that the dark side can draw from throughout the universe, Dooku hurls a jagged fragment of the durasteel table, Shmi Skywalker's gentle murmur I knew you would come for me, Anakin smashes it aside.»
- L1625 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «He decides that Dooku should lose the same hand he took.»
- L1629 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «And then Anakin takes Dooku's other hand as well.»
- L1631 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku crumples to his knees, face blank, mouth slack, and his weapon whirs through the air to the victor's hand, and Anakin finds his vision of the future happening before his eyes: two blades at Count Dooku's throat.»
- L1635 [SAID ABOUT] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku, cringing, shrinking with dread, still finds some hope in his heart that he is wrong, that Palpatine has not betrayed him, that this has all been proceeding according to plan-Until he hears "Good, Anakin!»
- L1653 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Years of Jedi training make Anakin hesitate; he looks down upon Dooku and sees not a Lord of the Sith but a beaten, broken, cringing old man.»

- L1697 [NARRATION] [Dooku / Darth Tyranus (age 83), 19 BBY] «Dooku's severed staring head bounced along the deck and rolled away, and Anakin woke up.»

- L1757 [NARRATION] [Dooku (referenced after death), 19 BBY] «But instead-He stared down at Dooku's severed head.»

- L2749 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «"It is." Mace looked grim. "We don't have even a preliminary estimate of civilian casualties."»
- L2755 [SAID ABOUT] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «I will, indeed." Palpatine touched Mace on the arm.»
- L2757 [SAID ABOUT] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «"You have always been of great value to me, Master Windu Thank you."»

- L2795 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «Mace accepted this news with a nod.»
- L2797 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «And while Palpatine answered, Mace Windu reached into the Force.»
- L2799 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «To Mace's Force perception, the world crystallized around them, becoming a gem of reality shot through with flaws and fault lines of possibility.»
- L2799 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «This was Mace's particular gift: to see how people and situations fit together in the Force, to find the shear planes that can cause them to break in useful ways, and to intuit what sort of strike would best make the cut.»

- L2815 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «Mace started walking toward the small-craft dock where the Jedi shuttle would land, and the others fell in with him.»
- L2817 [SAID ABOUT] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «They always do." He hoped Obi-Wan would take the hint and shut up about it; Mace had no desire to speak openly of the investigation in front of the Supreme Chancellor.»
- L2823 [SAID ABOUT] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «"Perhaps the Separatist leadership can restrain him, in exchange for certain ..." Mace let his gaze drift casually to a point somewhere above the Chancellor's head.»

- L3321 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan sat beside Mace Windu while they watched Yoda scan the report.»
- L3327 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «"The Jedi still have friends in the Senate," Mace Windu replied in his grim monotone, "for now."»
- L3331 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «Mace nodded. "My source expects passage by acclamation. Overwhelming passage. Perhaps as early as this afternoon."»

- L3355 [SAID ABOUT] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «That day may be today." Mace shot a frustrated look at Obi-Wan, who picked up his cue smoothly.»
- L3359 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «"I am convinced this is the next step in a plot aimed directly at the heart of the Jedi," Mace said. "This is a move toward our destruction. The dark side of the Force surrounds the Chancellor."»

- L3377 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «It's the only one we have." Mace looked deeply into Yoda's eyes, and into Obi-Wan's, and Obi-Wan could feel the heat in the Korun Master's gaze.»

- L4237 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «Mace and Yoda gazed at him steadily, and Obi-Wan had to lower his head.»

- L6021 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «Mace Windu had almost smiled. "I created Vaapad to answer my weakness: it channels my own darkness into a weapon of the light. Master Yoda's Ataro is also an answer to weakness: the limitations of reach and mobility imposed by his stature and his age. But for you? What weakness does Soresu answer?"»
- L6025 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «"That is so like you, Master Kenobi," the Korun Master had said, shaking his head. "I am called a great swordsman because I invented a lethal style; but who is greater, the creator of a killing form-or the master of the classic form?"»

**Ch16 Revelation**

- L6221 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «Mace Windu stood in the darkened comm center of Jedi Command, facing a life-sized holoscan of Yoda, projected from a concealed Wookiee comm center in the heart of a wroshyr tree on Kashyyyk.»
- L6223 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «"Minutes ago," Mace said, "we received confirmation from Utapau: Kenobi was successful. Grievous is dead."»
- L6227 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «"I will personally deliver the news of Grievous's death." Mace flexed his hands. "It will be up to the Chancellor to cede his emergency powers back over to the Senate."»
- L6235 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «"Too much of a risk," Mace replied. "I am the fourth."»
- L6239 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «"I will, Master. When the Republic is safe once more." Mace straightened. "We are waiting only for your vote."»
- L6243 [OWN LINE (alternation)] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «"And with you, Master."»
- L6247 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «Mace lowered his head and stood in the darkness and the silence.»
- L6251 [SAID ABOUT] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «"Master Windu . . . ?"»
- L6253 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «"Skywalker?" Mace was at his side in an instant. "What's wrong? Are you hurt?"»
- L6255 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «Anakin took Mace's arm in a grip of desperate strength, and used it like a crutch to haul himself upright.»
- L6265 [SAID ABOUT] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «"Listen to me-you have to listen to me-" Anakin sagged against him, shaking; Mace wrapped his arms around the young Jedi and guided him into the nearest chair.»
- L6265 [SAID ABOUT] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «"You can't-please, Master Windu, give me your word, promise me it'll be an arrest, promise you're not going to hurt him-"»
- L6271 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «Mace reached into the Force, opening the eye of his special gift of perception-What he found there froze his blood.»
- L6275 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «Mace said slowly, with the same sort of deliberate care he would use in examining an unknown type of bomb that might have the power to destroy the universe itself, "Anakin, look at me."»
- L6281 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «Mace frowned.»
- L6283 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «In the Force, Mace could feel the monster inside Anakin Skywalker, a real monster, too real, one that was eating him alive from the inside out.»
- L6289 [SAID ABOUT] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «"Master Windu ..." He spoke slowly, painfully, as though each word ripped away a raw hunk of his own flesh.»
- L6291 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «Mace stared at him.»
- L6299 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «This is the moment that defines Mace Windu.»
- L6309 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «Because Mace, too, has an attachment.»
- L6309 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «Mace has a secret love.»
- L6311 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «Mace Windu loves the Republic.»
- L6315 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «For Mace Windu, for all his life, for all the lives of a thousand years of Jedi before him, true civilization has had only one true name: the Republic.»
- L6335 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «Mace Windu's entire existence has become crystal so shot-through with flaws that the hammer of those nine words has crushed him to sand.»
- L6337 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «But because he is Mace Windu, he takes this blow without a change of expression.»
- L6339 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «Because he is Mace Windu, within a second the man of sand is stone once more: pure Jedi Master, weighing coldly the risk of facing the last Dark Lord of the Sith without the chosen one-Against the risk of facing the last Dark Lord of the Sith with a chosen one eaten alive by fear.»
- L6341 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «And because he is Mace Windu, the choice is no choice at all.»
- L6349 [SAID ABOUT] [Mace Windu, Jedi Master, 19 BBY] «"What are you going to do?" And it is the true measure of Mace Windu that, even now, he still is telling the truth when he says, "Only as much as I have to."»

- L6659 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu (Vaapad loop + shatterpoint, office duel), 19 BBY] «Sinking into Vaapad, Mace Windu fought for his life.»
- L6665 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu (Vaapad loop + shatterpoint, office duel), 19 BBY] «So did Mace's blade.»
- L6669 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu (Vaapad loop + shatterpoint, office duel), 19 BBY] «Mace Windu created this style, and he was its only living master.»
- L6673 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu (Vaapad loop + shatterpoint, office duel), 19 BBY] «Maybe he was still a bit flash-blind-the Korun Master seemed to be fading in and out of existence, half swallowed by a thickening black haze in which danced a meter-long bar of sunfire.»
- L6673 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu (Vaapad loop + shatterpoint, office duel), 19 BBY] «Mace pressed back the darkness with a relentless straight-ahead march; his own blade, that distinctive amethyst blaze that had been the final sight of so many evil beings across the galaxy, made a haze of its own: an oblate sphere of purple fire within which there seemed to be dozens of swords slashing in all directions at once.»
- L6687 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu (Vaapad loop + shatterpoint, office duel), 19 BBY] «Mace Windu was cutting loose.»
- L6689 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu (Vaapad loop + shatterpoint, office duel), 19 BBY] «Mace was deep in it now: submerged in Vaapad, swallowed by it, he no longer truly existed as an independent being.»
- L6695 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu (Vaapad loop + shatterpoint, office duel), 19 BBY] «There was a time when Mace Windu had feared the power of the dark; there was a time when he had feared the darkness in himself.»
- L6705 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu (Vaapad loop + shatterpoint, office duel), 19 BBY] «Which might have gone on forever, if Vaapad were Mace's only gift.»
- L6711 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu (Vaapad loop + shatterpoint, office duel), 19 BBY] «Mace had no need to look; the presence in the Force was familiar, and was as uplifting as sunlight breaking through a thunderhead.»
- L6715 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu (Vaapad loop + shatterpoint, office duel), 19 BBY] «Mace disengaged from the shadow's blade and leapt for the window; he slashed away the transparisteel with a single flourish.»
- L6725 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu (Vaapad loop + shatterpoint, office duel), 19 BBY] «Out where Mace could flick his blade in one precise arc and slash the shadow's lightsaber in half.»
- L6731 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Mace Windu (Vaapad loop + shatterpoint, office duel), 19 BBY] «"For all your power, you are no Jedi. All you are, my lord," Mace said evenly, staring past his blade, "is under arrest."»

- L6747 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu (Vaapad loop + shatterpoint, office duel), 19 BBY] «Lighting blasted the clouds above, and lightning blasted from Palpatine's hands, and Mace didn't have time to comprehend what Palpatine was talking about; he had time only to slip back into Vaapad and angle his blade to catch the forking arcs of pure, dazzling hatred that clawed toward him.»
- L6757 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Mace Windu (Vaapad loop + shatterpoint, office duel), 19 BBY] «"Anakin!" Mace called. His voice sounded distant, blurred, ; if it came from the bottom of a well. "Anakin, help me! This is your chance!"»

- L6761 [NARRATION] [Mace Windu (Vaapad loop + shatterpoint, office duel), 19 BBY] «That was when Mace finally understood.»

### 9.7 Yoda (169 quotes: 70 own lines, 33 said-about, 66 narration)

**Ch1 Anakin And Obi-Wan**

- L477 [NARRATION] [Yoda, Grand Master, 19 BBY] «And the only answers he ever has for these dead cold whispers are his memories of Obi-Wan's voice, or Yoda's.»
- L817 [NARRATION] [Yoda, Grand Master, 19 BBY] «Say what you will about the wisdom of ancient Master Yoda, or the deadly skill of grim Mace Windu, the courage of Ki-Adi-Mundi, or the subtle wiles of Shaak Ti; the greatness of all these Jedi is unquestioned, but it pales next to the legend that has grown around Kenobi and Skywalker.»

**Ch2 Dooku**

- L1005 [NARRATION] [Yoda, Grand Master, 19 BBY] «He'd said farewell to his former Padawan, Qui-Gon Jinn, now a legendary Master in his own right; he'd said farewell to his close friends on the Jedi Council, Mace Windu and the ancient Master Yoda; he'd said farewell to the Jedi Order itself.»
- L1051 [NARRATION] [Yoda, Grand Master, 19 BBY] «Such as-for most of his life, and to some extent even now-the Jedi, particularly Mace Windu and Yoda, both of whom had regarded him as their friend for so long that it had effectively blinded them to the truth of his activities.»

**Ch10 Masters**

- L3321 [NARRATION] [Yoda, Grand Master, 19 BBY] «Obi-Wan sat beside Mace Windu while they watched Yoda scan the report.»
- L3321 [NARRATION] [Yoda, Grand Master, 19 BBY] «Here in Yoda's simple living space within the Jedi Temple, every softly curving pod chair and knurled organiform table hummed with gentle, comforting power: the same warm strength that Obi-Wan remembered enfolding him even as an infant.»
- L3321 [NARRATION] [Yoda, Grand Master, 19 BBY] «These chambers had been Yoda's home for more than eight hundred years.»
- L3321 [NARRATION] [Yoda, Grand Master, 19 BBY] «Everything within them echoed with the harmonic resonance of Yoda's calm wisdom, tuned through centuries of his touch.»
- L3321 [NARRATION] [Yoda, Grand Master, 19 BBY] «To sit within Yoda's chambers was to inhale serenity; to Obi-Wan, this was a great gift in these troubled times.»
- L3323 [NARRATION] [Yoda, Grand Master, 19 BBY] «But when Yoda looked at them through the translucent shimmer of the holoprojected report on the contents of the latest amendment to the Security Act, his eyes were anything but calm: they had gone narrow and cold, and his ears had flattened back along his skull.»
- L3333 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Yoda, Grand Master, 19 BBY] «"The Chancellor's goal in this-unclear to me it is," Yoda said slowly.»
- L3355 [SAID ABOUT] [Yoda, Grand Master, 19 BBY] «"Master Yoda, that day may be coming sooner than any of us think.»
- L3363 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Yoda, Grand Master, 19 BBY] «"Speculation!" Yoda thumped the floor with his gimer stick, making his hoverchair bob gently. "On theories such as these we cannot rely. Proof' we need. Proof!"»
- L3375 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Yoda, Grand Master, 19 BBY] «Yoda shook his head. "To a dark place, this line of thought will lead us. Great care, we must take."»
- L3377 [SAID ABOUT (by Obi-Wan Kenobi)] [Yoda, Grand Master, 19 BBY] «It's the only one we have." Mace looked deeply into Yoda's eyes, and into Obi-Wan's, and Obi-Wan could feel the heat in the Korun Master's gaze.»

- L3385 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Yoda, Grand Master, 19 BBY] «"No disrespect, Master Yoda," Mace interrupted, "but that's a politician's argument.»
- L3387 [OWN LINE (tagged)] [Yoda, Grand Master, 19 BBY] «"Convinced it is right, I am not," Yoda said severely. "Working behind the scenes we should be, to uncover Lord Sidious! - L3391 [OWN LINE (beat)] [Yoda, Grand Master, 19 BBY] «"Have the advantage already, they do!" Yoda jabbed at him with his gimer stick. " Increase their advantage we will, if in haste we act!"»

- L3413 [SAID ABOUT (by Mace Windu)] [Yoda, Grand Master, 19 BBY] «"Yoda and I will remain on Coruscant, monitoring Palpatine's advisers and lackeys; we'll move against Sidi-ous the instant he is revealed.»
- L3547 [NARRATION] [Yoda, Grand Master, 19 BBY] «When a Jedi had a question about the deepest subtleties of the Force, there was one source to whom he could always turn; and so, first thing that morning, without even taking time to stop by his own quarters for a change of clothing, Anakin had gone to Yoda for advice.»
- L3549 [NARRATION] [Yoda, Grand Master, 19 BBY] «He'd been surprised by how graciously the ancient Jedi Master had invited him into his quarters, and by how patiently Yoda had listened to his stumbling attempts to explain his question without giving away his secret; Yoda had never made any attempt to conceal what had always seemed to Anakin to be a gruff»
- L3551 [NARRATION] [Yoda, Grand Master, 19 BBY] «But this morning, despite clearly having other things on his mind-even Anakin's Force perceptions, far from the most subtle had detected echoes of conflict and worry within the Masster's chamber-Yoda had simply offered Anakin a place on one of the softly rounded pod seats and suggested that they meditate together.»

## 10. Script verification
- **Method:** `_extract/tools.py` + `_extract/vparts.py`. Every guillemet-delimited quote is normalized: all quote, apostrophe and dash glyphs (' " ‘ ’ “ ” — – ` U+FFFD) are mapped to one placeholder, runs collapsed, whitespace collapsed, and `…` expanded. It is then matched as an exact substring of the normalized source `[local path]`. Elided quotes (` … `) and paragraph-joined quotes (` / `) are checked segment by segment, in order, within 3,000 characters.
- **Curated quotes (sections 1–8):** 1126 checked, **1126 verified, 0 failed.**
- **Quote bank (section 9):** 3868 checked, **3868 verified, 0 failed.**
- **Total:** 4994 quotes, **4994 verified, 0 failed.**
- Includes 200 hand-curated pronoun/epithet lines (§9.0), each also matched against its exact source line.
- Per-character bank counts (§9.1+): Anakin Skywalker / Darth Vader: 1199; Obi-Wan Kenobi: 899; Palpatine / Darth Sidious: 601; Count Dooku / Darth Tyranus: 202; General Grievous: 221; Mace Windu: 215; Yoda: 169; Padme Amidala: 162.
